

===== OUTPUT_generate_use_case OUTPUT =====
Use Case: Manage E-Shop Backend System
Primary Actor: User (Administrator/Customer)
Goal: Manage users, products, carts, reservations, and orders in an e-shop backend system while ensuring correct handling of current available stock.

---

**System Responsibilities:**
- Manage user accounts, including creation and deletion.
- Maintain product catalog with attributes (name, price, stock).
- Handle cart operations (add/remove products) and reservations.
- Process checkout to create orders with atomicity.
- Update order statuses following strict transition rules.
- Ensure stock consistency during reservations, checkouts, and cancellations.
- Generate globally unique and sequential order IDs.
- Provide access to current system state (users, products, stock, carts, orders).

---

**Preconditions:**
- The e-shop backend system is initialized and operational.
- No prior state corruption exists (e.g., stock inconsistencies).

---

**Postconditions:**
- Users, products, carts, reservations, and orders are in a consistent state.
- Stock levels reflect all successful operations (reservations, checkouts, cancellations).
- Order statuses adhere to the defined transition rules.
- Order IDs are unique and sequential.

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

### 1. Create User
1. Actor requests to create a user with a unique ID.
2. System validates the ID is not a duplicate.
3. System creates the user and an empty cart.
4. System confirms user creation.

### 2. Delete User
1. Actor requests to delete a user by ID.
2. System validates the user exists.
3. System removes the user’s cart and cancels all their reservations:
   - For each reserved product:
     - If the product exists in stock, the reserved quantity is returned to available stock.
     - If the product no longer exists, the reservation is removed without stock adjustment.
4. System deletes the user while preserving their orders.
5. System confirms user deletion.

### 3. Add Product
1. Actor requests to add a product with name, price, and stock.
2. System validates the input (e.g., non-negative stock).
3. If the product name already exists:
   - System increases the current available stock by the provided quantity.
   - System updates the product price.
4. If the product name does not exist:
   - System creates a new product with the provided attributes.
5. System confirms product addition/update.

### 4. Delete Product
1. Actor requests to delete a product by name.
2. System validates the product exists.
3. System removes the product from the current available stock system.
4. System preserves existing reservations and orders for the product.
5. System confirms product deletion.

### 5. Modify Cart
1. Actor requests to add/remove a product in their cart with a specified quantity.
2. System validates the user and product exist.
3. **Add Product to Cart:**
   - System checks if the requested quantity is available in stock.
   - If available, system reserves the quantity (deducts from available stock) and updates the cart.
   - If unavailable or quantity is zero, system ignores the request.
4. **Remove Product from Cart:**
   - System checks if the removal quantity is valid (≤ reserved amount).
   - If valid, system reduces/removes the reservation:
     - If the product exists in stock, the quantity is returned to available stock.
     - If the product no longer exists, the reservation is removed without stock adjustment.
   - If invalid, system ignores the request.
5. System confirms cart modification.

### 6. Perform Checkout
1. Actor requests to checkout their cart.
2. System validates the cart is not empty.
3. System verifies all reserved products still exist in the stock system.
4. System calculates the total price using current product prices.
5. System creates a new order with:
   - Status "pending".
   - A snapshot of product prices at checkout.
   - A globally unique ID (e.g., "SK00001").
6. System clears the cart and corresponding reservations.
7. System confirms checkout success.

### 7. Update Order Status
1. Actor requests to update an order’s status.
2. System validates the order exists and the new status is valid.
3. System checks the transition follows the allowed sequence:
   - `pending → processing → completed → shipped → delivered`
   - Any status → `cancelled` (except from `cancelled`).
   - From `delivered`, only → `cancelled`.
4. If transitioning to `cancelled`:
   - For each product in the order:
     - If the product exists in stock, system increases available stock by the ordered quantity.
     - If the product no longer exists, system recreates it with the price stored in the order.
5. System updates the order status.
6. System confirms status update.

---

**Extensions:**

### 1a. Create User
1a. Duplicate user ID → System ignores the request.

### 2a. Delete User
2a. User does not exist → System ignores the request.

### 3a. Add Product
3a. Invalid input (e.g., negative stock) → System ignores the request.

### 4a. Delete Product
4a. Product does not exist → System ignores the request.

### 5a. Modify Cart
5a. User or product does not exist → System ignores the request.
5b. Add product with zero quantity → System ignores the request.
5c. Add product with insufficient stock → System ignores the request.
5d. Remove product with invalid quantity → System ignores the request.

### 6a. Perform Checkout
6a. Cart is empty → System ignores the request.
6b. Reserved product no longer exists → System fails checkout and applies no changes.
6c. Any checkout failure → System rolls back all changes (atomicity).

### 7a. Update Order Status
7a. Order does not exist → System ignores the request.
7b. Invalid status transition → System ignores the request.
7c. Transition from `cancelled` → System ignores the request.

---

**Special Requirements:**
- All operations must maintain system consistency (e.g., stock levels, reservations).
- Order IDs must be globally unique, sequential, and never reused.
- Invalid inputs (e.g., negative quantities) must be ignored without side effects.
- The system must handle concurrent operations safely (implied by atomicity requirements).
- The implementation must use object-oriented design in Python.
===============================

---

Use Case: Manage E-Shop Backend System
Primary Actor: User (Administrator/Customer)
Goal: Manage users, products, carts, reservations, and orders in an e-shop backend system while ensuring correct handling of current available stock.

---

**System Responsibilities:**
- Manage user accounts, including creation and deletion.
- Maintain product catalog with attributes (name, price, stock).
- Handle cart operations (add/remove products) and reservations.
- Process checkout to create orders with atomicity.
- Update order statuses following strict transition rules.
- Ensure stock consistency during reservations, checkouts, and cancellations.
- Generate globally unique and sequential order IDs.
- Provide access to current system state (users, products, stock, carts, orders).

---

**Preconditions:**
- The e-shop backend system is initialized and operational.
- No prior state corruption exists (e.g., stock inconsistencies).

---

**Postconditions:**
- Users, products, carts, reservations, and orders are in a consistent state.
- Stock levels reflect all successful operations (reservations, checkouts, cancellations).
- Order statuses adhere to the defined transition rules.
- Order IDs are unique and sequential.

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

### 1. Create User
1. Actor requests to create a user with a unique ID.
2. System validates the ID is not a duplicate.
3. System creates the user and an empty cart.
4. System confirms user creation.

### 2. Delete User
1. Actor requests to delete a user by ID.
2. System validates the user exists.
3. System removes the user’s cart and cancels all their reservations:
   - For each reserved product:
     - If the product exists in stock, the reserved quantity is returned to available stock.
     - If the product no longer exists, the reservation is removed without stock adjustment.
4. System deletes the user while preserving their orders.
5. System confirms user deletion.

### 3. Add Product
1. Actor requests to add a product with name, price, and stock.
2. System validates the input (e.g., non-negative stock).
3. If the product name already exists:
   - System increases the current available stock by the provided quantity.
   - System updates the product price.
4. If the product name does not exist:
   - System creates a new product with the provided attributes.
5. System confirms product addition/update.

### 4. Delete Product
1. Actor requests to delete a product by name.
2. System validates the product exists.
3. System removes the product from the current available stock system while preserving all existing reservations and orders for the product.
4. System confirms product deletion.

### 5. Modify Cart
1. Actor requests to add/remove a product in their cart with a specified quantity.
2. System validates the user and product exist.
3. **Add Product to Cart:**
   - System checks if the requested quantity is available in stock.
   - If the product is already in the cart, system updates the reserved quantity (increases by the specified amount).
   - If the product is not in the cart, system reserves the quantity (deducts from available stock) and adds it to the cart.
   - If unavailable or quantity is zero, system ignores the request.
4. **Remove Product from Cart:**
   - System checks if the removal quantity is valid (≤ reserved amount).
   - If valid, system reduces/removes the reservation:
     - If the product exists in stock, the quantity is returned to available stock.
     - If the product no longer exists, the reservation is removed without stock adjustment.
   - If invalid, system ignores the request.
5. System confirms cart modification.

### 6. Perform Checkout
1. Actor requests to checkout their cart.
2. System validates the cart is not empty.
3. System verifies all reserved products still exist in the stock system. If any reserved product no longer exists, system fails checkout and applies no changes.
4. System calculates the total price using current product prices.
5. System creates a new order with:
   - Status "pending".
   - A snapshot of product prices at checkout.
   - A globally unique and sequential ID (e.g., "SK00001").
6. System clears the cart and corresponding reservations.
7. System confirms checkout success.

### 7. Update Order Status
1. Actor requests to update an order’s status.
2. System validates the order exists and the new status is valid.
3. System checks the transition follows the allowed sequence:
   - `pending → processing → completed → shipped → delivered`
   - Any status → `cancelled` (except from `cancelled`).
   - From `delivered`, only → `cancelled` (no other transitions allowed).
4. If transitioning to `cancelled`:
   - For each product in the order:
     - If the product exists in stock, system increases available stock by the ordered quantity.
     - If the product no longer exists, system recreates it with the price stored in the order.
5. System updates the order status.
6. System confirms status update.

---

**Extensions:**

### 1a. Create User
1a. Duplicate user ID → System ignores the request.

### 2a. Delete User
2a. User does not exist → System ignores the request.

### 3a. Add Product
3a. Invalid input (e.g., negative stock) → System ignores the request.

### 4a. Delete Product
4a. Product does not exist → System ignores the request.

### 5a. Modify Cart
5a. User or product does not exist → System ignores the request.
5b. Add product with zero quantity → System ignores the request.
5c. Add product with insufficient stock → System ignores the request.
5d. Remove product with invalid quantity → System ignores the request.

### 6a. Perform Checkout
6a. Cart is empty → System ignores the request.
6b. Reserved product no longer exists → System fails checkout and applies no changes.
6c. Any checkout failure → System rolls back all changes (atomicity).

### 7a. Update Order Status
7a. Order does not exist → System ignores the request.
7b. Invalid status transition → System ignores the request.
7c. Transition from `cancelled` → System ignores the request.

---

**Special Requirements:**
- All operations must maintain system consistency (e.g., stock levels, reservations).
- Order IDs must be globally unique, sequential, and never reused.
- Invalid inputs (e.g., negative quantities) must be ignored without side effects.
- The system must handle concurrent operations safely (implied by atomicity requirements).
- The implementation must use object-oriented design in Python.

---

Here's the revised Use Case with corrections applied based on the valid review issues:

---

Use Case: Manage E-Shop Backend System
Primary Actor: User (Administrator/Customer)
Goal: Manage users, products, carts, reservations, and orders in an e-shop backend system while ensuring correct handling of current available stock.

---

**System Responsibilities:**
- Manage user accounts, including creation and deletion.
- Maintain product catalog with attributes (name, price, stock).
- Handle cart operations (add/remove products) and reservations.
- Process checkout to create orders with atomicity.
- Update order statuses following strict transition rules.
- Ensure stock consistency during reservations, checkouts, and cancellations.
- Generate globally unique and sequential order IDs.
- Provide access to current system state (users, products, stock, carts, orders).

---

**Preconditions:**
- The e-shop backend system is initialized and operational.
- No prior state corruption exists (e.g., stock inconsistencies).

---

**Postconditions:**
- Users, products, carts, reservations, and orders are in a consistent state.
- Stock levels reflect all successful operations (reservations, checkouts, cancellations).
- Order statuses adhere to the defined transition rules.
- Order IDs are unique and sequential.

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

### 1. Create User
1. Actor requests to create a user with a unique ID.
2. System validates the ID is not a duplicate.
3. System creates the user and an empty cart.
4. System confirms user creation.

### 2. Delete User
1. Actor requests to delete a user by ID.
2. System validates the user exists.
3. System removes the user’s cart and cancels all their reservations:
   - For each reserved product:
     - If the product exists in stock, the reserved quantity is returned to available stock.
     - If the product no longer exists, the reservation is removed without stock adjustment.
4. System deletes the user while preserving their orders (orders remain unaffected and accessible in the system).
5. System confirms user deletion.

### 3. Add Product
1. Actor requests to add a product with name, price, and stock (zero stock allowed).
2. System validates the input (e.g., non-negative stock).
3. If the product name already exists:
   - System increases the current available stock by the provided quantity.
   - System updates the product price to the provided price.
4. If the product name does not exist:
   - System creates a new product with the provided attributes.
5. System confirms product addition/update.

### 4. Delete Product
1. Actor requests to delete a product by name.
2. System validates the product exists.
3. System removes the product from the current available stock system while preserving all existing reservations and orders for the product (reservations remain tracked but no longer affect available stock; reserved quantities are still deducted from the deleted product's stock record).
4. System confirms product deletion.

### 5. Modify Cart
1. Actor requests to add/remove a product in their cart with a specified quantity.
2. System validates the user and product exist.
3. **Add Product to Cart:**
   - If the requested quantity is zero, system ignores the request.
   - System checks if the requested quantity is available in stock.
   - If the product is already in the cart, system updates the reserved quantity (increases by the specified amount).
   - If the product is not in the cart, system reserves the quantity (deducts from available stock, making it unavailable to other users) and adds it to the cart.
   - If unavailable, system ignores the request.
4. **Remove Product from Cart:**
   - System checks if the removal quantity is valid (≤ reserved amount).
   - If valid, system reduces/removes the reservation:
     - If the product exists in stock, the quantity is returned to available stock.
     - If the product no longer exists, the reservation is removed without stock adjustment.
   - If invalid, system ignores the request.
5. System confirms cart modification.

### 6. Perform Checkout
1. Actor requests to checkout their cart.
2. System validates the cart is not empty.
3. System verifies all reserved products still exist in the stock system and that the reserved quantities are still available. If any reserved product no longer exists or the reserved quantity is unavailable, system fails checkout and applies no changes.
4. System calculates the total price using current product prices.
5. System creates a new order with:
   - Status "pending".
   - A snapshot of product prices at checkout.
   - A globally unique and sequential ID (e.g., "SK00001").
6. System clears the cart and corresponding reservations.
7. System confirms checkout success.

### 7. Update Order Status
1. Actor requests to update an order’s status.
2. System validates the order exists and the new status is valid.
3. System checks the transition follows the allowed sequence:
   - `pending → processing → completed → shipped → delivered`
   - Any status → `cancelled` (except from `cancelled`; no transitions from `cancelled` are allowed).
   - From `delivered`, only → `cancelled` (no other transitions allowed).
4. If transitioning to `cancelled`:
   - For each product in the order:
     - If the product exists in stock, system increases available stock by the ordered quantity.
     - If the product no longer exists, system recreates it with the price and quantity stored in the order.
5. System updates the order status.
6. System confirms status update.

---

**Extensions:**

### 1a. Create User
1a. Duplicate user ID → System ignores the request.

### 2a. Delete User
2a. User does not exist → System ignores the request.

### 3a. Add Product
3a. Invalid input (e.g., negative stock) → System ignores the request.

### 4a. Delete Product
4a. Product does not exist → System ignores the request.

### 5a. Modify Cart
5a. User or product does not exist → System ignores the request.
5b. Add product with zero quantity → System ignores the request.
5c. Add product with insufficient stock → System ignores the request.
5d. Remove product with invalid quantity → System ignores the request.

### 6a. Perform Checkout
6a. Cart is empty → System ignores the request.
6b. Reserved product no longer exists or reserved quantity unavailable → System fails checkout and applies no changes.
6c. Any checkout failure → System rolls back all changes (atomicity).

### 7a. Update Order Status
7a. Order does not exist → System ignores the request.
7b. Invalid status transition → System ignores the request.
7c. Transition from `cancelled` → System ignores the request.

---

**Special Requirements:**
- All operations must maintain system consistency (e.g., stock levels, reservations).
- Order IDs must be globally unique, sequential, and never reused.
- Invalid inputs (e.g., negative quantities) must be ignored without side effects.
- The system must handle concurrent operations safely (implied by atomicity requirements).
- Reserved quantities must not be available to other users during the reservation period.
- The implementation must use object-oriented design in Python.
===============================

===== OUTPUT_review_architecture OUTPUT =====
{
  "score": 0.85,
  "issues": [
    "The `EShopBackend` class does not track reservations explicitly as a separate attribute, which is required for maintaining stock consistency and handling reservations during user deletion, cart modification, and checkout. Reservations are mentioned in methods but not persisted as a distinct state.",
    "The `delete_user` method does not explicitly handle the case where a product in the user's reservations no longer exists in the `products` dictionary. While the steps mention this, the absence of a reservations attribute makes it unclear how this is tracked and validated.",
    "The `modify_cart` method's steps for 'add' operation do not explicitly state that the reserved quantity must be deducted from the available stock immediately upon reservation, which is a critical requirement for stock consistency.",
    "The `perform_checkout` method does not explicitly state that the snapshot of product prices at checkout must include the product name, quantity, and price. The `Order` class's `products` attribute is described as a dictionary, but the exact structure is not enforced in the method steps.",
    "The `update_order_status` method does not explicitly state that transitioning to 'cancelled' from 'delivered' is allowed, though it is implied. This should be explicitly mentioned in the steps for clarity and correctness.",
    "The `EShopBackend` class lacks an attribute to track the next sequential order ID format (e.g., 'SK00001'). While `next_order_id` is present, the format and prefix are not enforced in the architecture, which could lead to inconsistencies in order ID generation.",
    "The `get_system_state` method does not explicitly include reservations in its output description, which is required for full system state visibility as per the Use Case."
  ]
}

===== OUTPUT_review_architecture OUTPUT =====
{
  "score": 0.85,
  "issues": [
    "The `perform_checkout` method does not explicitly verify that the reserved quantities in the cart match the available stock at checkout time. While the description mentions 'verifying all reserved products are still available,' it does not explicitly state that the reserved quantity must match the available stock (e.g., no partial stock changes after reservation). This could lead to inconsistencies if stock is modified between reservation and checkout.",
    "The `update_order_status` method does not explicitly handle the case where a product in a cancelled order no longer exists in the stock system. While the description mentions recreating the product, it does not specify that the product should be recreated with the exact price and quantity stored in the order snapshot, which is a critical requirement.",
    "The `delete_product` method does not explicitly state that existing reservations for the deleted product should remain tracked but no longer affect available stock. This could lead to confusion about the state of reservations post-deletion.",
    "The `modify_cart` method does not explicitly handle the case where a product is removed from the cart and the product no longer exists in the stock system. While the description mentions removing the reservation without stock adjustment, it does not clarify that the reservation should be completely removed from the system.",
    "The `EShopBackend` class lacks an explicit attribute to track the current available stock (i.e., stock not reserved in any cart). While the `get_system_state` method returns this information, the absence of a dedicated attribute could lead to inconsistencies in stock calculations during operations like `modify_cart` or `perform_checkout`.",
    "The `perform_checkout` method does not explicitly state that the order ID must be generated in a thread-safe manner to ensure uniqueness and sequentiality in a concurrent environment, which is implied by the 'globally unique and sequential' requirement.",
    "The `update_order_status` method does not explicitly state that transitions from 'cancelled' must be ignored, though this is implied by the 'terminal state' requirement. Explicitly stating this in the steps would improve clarity and prevent implementation errors."
  ]
}

===== OUTPUT_review_architecture OUTPUT =====
{
  "score": 0.85,
  "issues": [
    "The `perform_checkout` method does not explicitly ensure thread-safety for order ID generation and incrementing `next_order_id`, which is required for atomicity and uniqueness in concurrent environments (Special Requirement: 'Order ID generation must be thread-safe').",
    "The `perform_checkout` method does not explicitly verify that the reserved quantities in the cart match the available stock in `available_stock` at checkout time, which is required to ensure stock consistency (Special Requirement: 'Reserved quantities must already be deducted from available stock at the time of reservation and must match available stock at checkout.').",
    "The `update_order_status` method does not explicitly handle the case where a product is recreated with the exact price and quantity from the order snapshot when transitioning to 'cancelled'. The steps mention recreating the product but do not ensure the stock is set to the ordered quantity (Special Requirement: 'If the product no longer exists, recreate it with the exact price and quantity stored in the order snapshot.').",
    "The `modify_cart` method does not explicitly handle the case where a product is removed from the cart and the product no longer exists in the stock system. While the steps mention removing the reservation, they do not clarify that the reservation must be completely removed from the system (Special Requirement: 'If the product no longer exists, remove the reservation without adjusting stock.').",
    "The `delete_user` method does not explicitly ensure that reservations are removed from the `reservations` dictionary after returning stock, which could lead to inconsistent state (Special Requirement: 'Remove all reservations associated with the user from the reservations dictionary.').",
    "The `add_product` method does not explicitly validate that the `price` is non-negative, which could lead to invalid product states (Special Requirement: 'Invalid inputs (e.g., negative stock) must be ignored without side effects.').",
    "The `Order` class does not include a method or attribute to enforce the strict status transition rules, which could lead to invalid state transitions if not handled externally (Special Requirement: 'Status transitions must strictly follow the defined rules.')."
  ]
}

def delete_user(self, user_id: str) -> bool:
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False

    # Get user's reservations
    user_reservations = self.reservations.get(user_id, {})

    # Process each reservation
    for product_name, quantity in user_reservations.items():
        if product_name in self.products:
            # Return stock to available_stock if product exists
            self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity

    # Remove user from reservations
    if user_id in self.reservations:
        del self.reservations[user_id]

    # Remove user from users
    del self.users[user_id]

    return True

        return False

        product = self.products[name]
        product.price = price
        product.stock += stock
        self.available_stock[name] += stock
    else:
        product = Product(name, price, stock)
        self.products[name] = product
        self.available_stock[name] = stock

    return True

===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopBackend.modify_cart: 
def modify_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity, operation):
    if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.products:
        return False

    if operation == 'add':
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            if product_name not in self.available_stock or self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
                return False

            if user_id in self.users[user_id].cart and product_name in self.users[user_id].cart:
                self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] += quantity
            else:
                if user_id not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                    self.users[user_id].cart = {}
                self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] = quantity

            if product_name not in self.reservations:
                self.reservations[product_name] = {}
            if user_id not in self.reservations[product_name]:
                self.reservations[product_name][user_id] = 0
            self.reservations[product_name][user_id] += quantity

            self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity
            return True

    elif operation == 'remove':
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            if user_id not in self.users[user_id].cart or product_name not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                return False

            if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] < quantity:
                return False

            if product_name in self.products:
                if product_name not in self.available_stock:
                    self.available_stock[product_name] = 0
                self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
            else:
                if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                    if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                        del self.reservations[product_name]

            self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] -= quantity
            if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] == 0:
                del self.users[user_id].cart[product_name]
            return True

    return False
===============================


===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopBackend.perform_checkout: 
def perform_checkout(self, user_id):
    # Check if user exists
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False, None

    # Get user's cart
    cart = self.users[user_id].cart
    if not cart:
        return False, None

    # Acquire lock for thread-safe operations
    with self.lock:
        # Verify all products in cart are available with matching quantities
        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            if product_name not in self.products or product_name not in self.available_stock:
                return False, None
            if self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
                return False, None

        # Calculate total price
        total_price = sum(
            self.products[product_name].price * quantity
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
        )

        # Generate order ID
        order_id = f"{self.order_id_prefix}{str(self.next_order_id).zfill(self.order_id_digits)}"

        # Create order snapshot
        order_snapshot = {
            product_name: {
                'quantity': quantity,
                'price': self.products[product_name].price
            }
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
        }

        # Create and store order
        order = Order(
            order_id=order_id,
            user_id=user_id,
            status='pending',
            total_price=total_price,
            products=order_snapshot
        )
        self.orders[order_id] = order

        # Clear cart and reservations
        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name]
            self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity

        # Clear the cart
        self.users[user_id].cart.clear()

        # Increment order ID
        self.next_order_id += 1

    return True, order_id
===============================


===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopBackend.update_order_status: 
def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> bool:
    # Check if the order exists
    if order_id not in self.orders:
        return False

    order = self.orders[order_id]
    current_status = order.status

    # Define valid status transitions
    valid_transitions = {
        'pending': ['processing'],
        'processing': ['completed'],
        'completed': ['shipped'],
        'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
        'delivered': ['cancelled'],
        'cancelled': []
    }

    # Check if the transition is valid
    if new_status not in valid_transitions.get(current_status, []):
        return False

    # Handle cancellation separately
    if new_status == 'cancelled':
        for product_name, quantity in order.snapshot.items():
            if product_name in self.products:
                # Product exists, increase available stock
                self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
            else:
                # Product doesn't exist, recreate it with order snapshot details
                product_price = order.product_prices.get(product_name, 0.0)
                self.products[product_name] = Product(product_name, product_price, quantity)
                self.available_stock[product_name] = quantity

    # Update the order status
    order.status = new_status
    return True
===============================


===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopBackend.get_system_state: 
def get_system_state(self):
    """Provides access to the current system state.

    Returns:
        dict: Dictionary containing the current system state with keys:
            - 'users': Current users and their carts
            - 'products': Current products with names, prices, and available stock
            - 'available_stock': Products not reserved in any cart
            - 'carts': Carts and reservations of all current users
            - 'reservations': All current reservations in the system
            - 'orders': All current orders and their statuses
    """
    system_state = {
        "users": {user_id: user.cart for user_id, user in self.users.items()},
        "products": {
            name: {
                "price": product.price,
                "stock": product.stock
            } for name, product in self.products.items()
        },
        "available_stock": self.available_stock.copy(),
        "carts": {user_id: user.cart for user_id, user in self.users.items()},
        "reservations": self.reservations.copy(),
        "orders": {order_id: order.status for order_id, order in self.orders.items()}
    }
    return system_state

def is_valid_transition(self, new_status):
    """Checks if a status transition is valid."""
    if self.status in self.valid_transitions and new_status in self.valid_transitions[self.status]:
        return True
    return False

def delete_user(self, user_id: str) -> bool:
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False

    # Get user's reservations
    user_reservations = {}
    for product_name, user_reservation in self.reservations.items():
        if user_id in user_reservation:
            user_reservations[product_name] = user_reservation[user_id]

    # Process each reservation
    for product_name, quantity in user_reservations.items():
        if product_name in self.products:
            # Return stock to available_stock if product exists
            self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity

    # Remove user from reservations
    for product_name in user_reservations.keys():
        if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
            del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
            if not self.reservations[product_name]:  # Clean up empty product reservations
                del self.reservations[product_name]

    # Remove user from users
    del self.users[user_id]

    return True

def modify_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity, operation):
    if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.products:
        return False

    if operation == 'add':
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            if product_name not in self.available_stock or self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
                return False

            if user_id not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                self.users[user_id].cart = {}
            if product_name in self.users[user_id].cart:
                self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] += quantity
            else:
                self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] = quantity

            if product_name not in self.reservations:
                self.reservations[product_name] = {}
            if user_id not in self.reservations[product_name]:
                self.reservations[product_name][user_id] = 0
            self.reservations[product_name][user_id] += quantity

            self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity
            return True

    elif operation == 'remove':
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            if user_id not in self.users[user_id].cart or product_name not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                return False

            if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] < quantity:
                return False

            if product_name in self.products:
                if product_name not in self.available_stock:
                    self.available_stock[product_name] = 0
                self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
            else:
                if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                    if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                        del self.reservations[product_name]

            self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] -= quantity
            if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] == 0:
                del self.users[user_id].cart[product_name]
            return True

    return False

===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopBackend.perform_checkout: 
def perform_checkout(self, user_id):
    # Check if user exists
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False, None

    # Get user's cart
    cart = self.users[user_id].cart
    if not cart:
        return False, None

    # Acquire lock for thread-safe operations
    with self.lock:
        # Verify all products in cart are available with matching quantities
        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            if product_name not in self.products or product_name not in self.available_stock:
                return False, None
            if self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
                return False, None

        # Calculate total price
        total_price = sum(
            self.products[product_name].price * quantity
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
        )

        # Generate order ID
        order_id = f"{self.order_id_prefix}{str(self.next_order_id).zfill(self.order_id_digits)}"

        # Create order snapshot
        order_snapshot = {
            product_name: {
                'quantity': quantity,
                'price_at_checkout': self.products[product_name].price
            }
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
        }

        # Create and store order
        order = Order(
            order_id=order_id,
            user_id=user_id,
            total_price=total_price,
            products=order_snapshot
        )
        self.orders[order_id] = order

        # Clear cart and reservations
        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name]
            self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity

        # Clear the cart
        self.users[user_id].cart.clear()

        # Increment order ID
        self.next_order_id += 1

    return True, order_id
===============================


===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopBackend.update_order_status: 
def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> bool:
    # Check if the order exists
    if order_id not in self.orders:
        return False

    order = self.orders[order_id]
    current_status = order.status

    # Define valid status transitions
    valid_transitions = {
        'pending': ['processing'],
        'processing': ['completed'],
        'completed': ['shipped'],
        'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
        'delivered': ['cancelled'],
        'cancelled': []
    }

    # Check if the transition is valid
    if new_status not in valid_transitions.get(current_status, []):
        return False

    # Handle cancellation separately
    if new_status == 'cancelled':
        with self.lock:
            for product_name, quantity in order.items.items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    # Product exists, increase available stock
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                else:
                    # Product doesn't exist, recreate it with order snapshot details
                    product_price = order.prices.get(product_name, 0.0)
                    self.products[product_name] = Product(product_name, product_price, quantity)
                    self.available_stock[product_name] = quantity

    # Update the order status
    order.status = new_status
    return True

    """Provides access to the current system state.

    Returns:
        dict: Dictionary containing the current system state with keys:
            - 'users': Current users and their carts
            - 'products': Current products with names, prices, and available stock
            - 'available_stock': Products not reserved in any cart
            - 'carts': Carts and reservations of all current users
            - 'reservations': All current reservations in the system
            - 'orders': All current orders and their statuses
    """
    system_state = {
        "users": {user_id: user.cart for user_id, user in self.users.items()},
        "products": {
            name: {
                "price": product.price,
                "stock": product.stock
            } for name, product in self.products.items()
        },
        "available_stock": self.available_stock.copy(),
        "carts": {user_id: user.cart for user_id, user in self.users.items()},
        "reservations": self.reservations.copy(),
        "orders": {order_id: order.status for order_id, order in self.orders.items()}
    }
    return system_state

Regenerated code for EShopBackend.add_product: 
def add_product(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int) -> bool:
    if not isinstance(name, str) or not name.strip() or price < 0 or stock < 0:
        return False

    with self.lock:
        if name in self.products:
            product = self.products[name]
            product.price = price
            product.stock += stock
            self.available_stock[name] += stock
        else:
            product = Product(name, price, stock)
            self.products[name] = product
            self.available_stock[name] = stock

    return True

===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopBackend.modify_cart: 
def modify_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity, operation):
    if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.products:
        return False

    if operation == 'add':
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            if product_name not in self.available_stock or self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
                return False

            if product_name not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] = quantity
            else:
                self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] += quantity

            if product_name not in self.reservations:
                self.reservations[product_name] = {}
            if user_id not in self.reservations[product_name]:
                self.reservations[product_name][user_id] = 0
            self.reservations[product_name][user_id] += quantity

            self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity
            return True

    elif operation == 'remove':
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            if product_name not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                return False

            if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] < quantity:
                return False

            if product_name in self.products:
                if product_name not in self.available_stock:
                    self.available_stock[product_name] = 0
                self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
            else:
                if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                    if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                        del self.reservations[product_name]

            self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] -= quantity
            if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] == 0:
                del self.users[user_id].cart[product_name]
            return True

    return False
===============================


===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopBackend.update_order_status: 
def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> bool:
    # Check if the order exists
    if order_id not in self.orders:
        return False

    order = self.orders[order_id]
    current_status = order.status

    # Define valid status transitions
    valid_transitions = {
        'pending': ['processing'],
        'processing': ['completed'],
        'completed': ['shipped'],
        'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
        'delivered': ['cancelled'],
        'cancelled': []
    }

    # Check if the new status is valid
    allowed_statuses = ['pending', 'processing', 'completed', 'shipped', 'delivered', 'cancelled']
    if new_status not in allowed_statuses:
        return False

    # Check if the transition is valid
    if new_status not in valid_transitions.get(current_status, []):
        return False

    # Handle cancellation separately
    if new_status == 'cancelled':
        with self.lock:
            for product_name, item_data in order.items.items():
                quantity = item_data['quantity']
                price_at_checkout = item_data['price_at_checkout']

                if product_name in self.products:
                    # Product exists, increase available stock
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                    # Update product stock
                    self.products[product_name].stock += quantity
                else:
                    # Product doesn't exist, recreate it with order snapshot details
                    self.products[product_name] = Product(product_name, price_at_checkout, quantity)
                    self.available_stock[product_name] = quantity

    # Update the order status
    order.status = new_status
    return True
===============================

Regenerated code for EShopBackend.get_system_state: 
def get_system_state(self):
    """Provides access to the current system state.

    Returns:
        dict: Dictionary containing the current system state with keys:
            - 'users': Current users and their carts
            - 'products': Current products with names, prices, and available stock
            - 'available_stock': Products not reserved in any cart
            - 'carts': Carts and reservations of all current users
            - 'reservations': All current reservations in the system
            - 'orders': All current orders and their statuses
    """
    system_state = {
        "users": {user_id: user.cart for user_id, user in self.users.items()},
        "products": {
            name: {
                "price": product.price,
                "stock": product.stock,
                "available_stock": self.available_stock.get(name, 0)
            } for name, product in self.products.items()
        },
        "available_stock": self.available_stock.copy(),
        "carts": {user_id: user.cart for user_id, user in self.users.items()},
        "reservations": self.reservations.copy(),
        "orders": {order_id: order.status for order_id, order in self.orders.items()}
    }
    return system_state

def modify_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity, operation):
    if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.products:
        return False

    if operation == 'add':
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            if product_name not in self.available_stock or self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:

            if product_name not in self.users[user_id].cart:

            else:
                self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] += quantity

                del self.users[user_id].cart[product_name]
            return True

Regenerated code for EShopBackend.update_order_status: 
def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> bool:
    # Check if the order exists
    if order_id not in self.orders:
        return False

    order = self.orders[order_id]
    current_status = order.status

    # Check if transition is from 'cancelled' (explicitly ignore)
    if current_status == 'cancelled':
        return False

    # Use Order's is_valid_transition method for validation
    if not order.is_valid_transition(new_status):
        return False

    # Handle cancellation separately
    if new_status == 'cancelled':
        with self.lock:
            for product_name, item_data in order.items.items():
                quantity = item_data['quantity']
                price_at_checkout = item_data['price_at_checkout']

                if product_name in self.products:
                    # Product exists, increase available stock
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                    # Update product stock
                    self.products[product_name].stock += quantity
                else:
                    # Product doesn't exist, recreate it with order snapshot details
                    self.products[product_name] = Product(product_name, price_at_checkout, quantity)
                    self.available_stock[product_name] = quantity

                # Update reservations if any exist
                if product_name in self.reservations:
                    for user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                        if user_id in self.users and product_name in self.users[user_id].cart:
                            # Adjust user's cart quantity if reservation exists
                            self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] -= quantity
                            if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] <= 0:
                                del self.users[user_id].cart[product_name]

    # Update the order status
    order.status = new_status
    return True
===============================

            - 'products': Current products with names, prices, and available stock

        "products": {

                "price": product.price,
                "stock": product.stock,

            } for name, product in self.products.items()

        "available_stock": self.available_stock.copy(),
        "reservations": self.reservations.copy(),

    return system_state

Regenerated code for EShopBackend.delete_user: 
def delete_user(self, user_id: str) -> bool:
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False

    # Get user's reservations
    user_reservations = {}
    for product_name, user_reservation in self.reservations.items():
        if user_id in user_reservation:
            user_reservations[product_name] = user_reservation[user_id]

    # Process each reservation
    for product_name, quantity in user_reservations.items():
        if product_name in self.products:
            # Return stock to available_stock if product exists
            self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
        # If product doesn't exist, skip stock adjustment (as per specification)

    # Remove user from reservations
    for product_name in user_reservations.keys():
        if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
            del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
            if not self.reservations[product_name]:  # Clean up empty product reservations
                del self.reservations[product_name]

    # Remove user from users
    del self.users[user_id]

    return True

Regenerated code for EShopBackend.perform_checkout: 
def perform_checkout(self, user_id):
    # Check if user exists
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False, None

    # Get user's cart
    cart = self.users[user_id].cart
    if not cart:
        return False, None

    # Acquire lock for thread-safe operations
    with self.lock:
        # Verify all products in cart are available with matching quantities
        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            if product_name not in self.products or product_name not in self.available_stock:
                return False, None
            if self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
                return False, None
            # Verify reserved quantity matches cart quantity
            if (product_name not in self.reservations or
                user_id not in self.reservations[product_name] or
                self.reservations[product_name][user_id] != quantity):
                return False, None

        # Calculate total price
        total_price = sum(
            self.products[product_name].price * quantity
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
        )

        # Generate order ID
        order_id = f"{self.order_id_prefix}{str(self.next_order_id).zfill(self.order_id_digits)}"

        # Create order snapshot
        order_snapshot = {
            product_name: {
                'quantity': quantity,
                'price_at_checkout': self.products[product_name].price
            }
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
        }

        # Create and store order
        order = Order(
            order_id=order_id,
            user_id=user_id,
            total_price=total_price,
            products=order_snapshot
        )
        self.orders[order_id] = order

        # Clear cart and reservations
        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name]
            self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity

        # Clear the cart
        self.users[user_id].cart.clear()

        # Increment order ID
        self.next_order_id += 1

    return True, order_id

def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> bool:
    # Check if the order exists
    if order_id not in self.orders:
        return False

    order = self.orders[order_id]
    current_status = order.status

    # Check if transition is from 'cancelled' (explicitly ignore)
    if current_status == 'cancelled':
        return False

    # Use Order's is_valid_transition method for validation
    if not order.is_valid_transition(new_status):
        return False

    # Handle cancellation separately
    if new_status == 'cancelled':
        with self.lock:
            for product_name, item_data in order.products.items():
                quantity = item_data['quantity']
                price_at_checkout = item_data['price_at_checkout']

                if product_name in self.products:
                    # Product exists, increase available stock
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                    # Update product stock
                    self.products[product_name].stock += quantity
                else:
                    # Product doesn't exist, recreate it with order snapshot details
                    self.products[product_name] = Product(product_name, price_at_checkout, quantity)
                    self.available_stock[product_name] = quantity

    # Update the order status
    order.status = new_status
    return True

def delete_user(self, user_id: str) -> bool:
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False

    with self.lock:
        # Get user's reservations
        user_reservations = {}
        for product_name, user_reservation in self.reservations.items():
            if user_id in user_reservation:
                user_reservations[product_name] = user_reservation[user_id]

        # Process each reservation
        for product_name, quantity in user_reservations.items():
            if product_name in self.products:
                # Return stock to available_stock if product exists
                self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
            # If product doesn't exist, skip stock adjustment (as per specification)

        # Remove user from reservations
        for product_name in user_reservations.keys():
            if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                if not self.reservations[product_name]:  # Clean up empty product reservations
                    del self.reservations[product_name]

        # Remove user from users
        del self.users[user_id]

    return True

def add_product(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int) -> bool:
    if not isinstance(name, str) or not name.strip() or price < 0 or stock < 0:
        return False

    with self.lock:
        if name in self.products:
            product = self.products[name]
            product.price = price
            product.stock += stock
            self.available_stock[name] += stock
        else:
            product = Product(name, price, stock)
            self.products[name] = product
            self.available_stock[name] = stock

    return True

Regenerated code for EShopBackend.modify_cart: 
def modify_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity, operation):
    if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.products:
        return False

    if operation == 'add':
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            if product_name not in self.available_stock or self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
                return False

            if product_name not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] = quantity
            else:
                self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] += quantity

            if product_name not in self.reservations:
                self.reservations[product_name] = {}
            if user_id not in self.reservations[product_name]:
                self.reservations[product_name][user_id] = 0
            self.reservations[product_name][user_id] += quantity

            self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity
            return True

    elif operation == 'remove':
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            if product_name not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                return False

            if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] < quantity:
                return False

            if product_name in self.products:
                if product_name not in self.available_stock:
                    self.available_stock[product_name] = 0
                self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
            else:
                if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                    if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                        del self.reservations[product_name]

            self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] -= quantity
            if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] <= 0:
                del self.users[user_id].cart[product_name]
            return True

    return False

===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopBackend.perform_checkout: 
def perform_checkout(self, user_id):
    # Check if user exists
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False, None

    # Get user's cart
    cart = self.users[user_id].cart
    if not cart:
        return False, None

    # Acquire lock for thread-safe operations
    with self.lock:
        # Verify all products in cart are available with matching quantities
        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            if product_name not in self.products or product_name not in self.available_stock:
                return False, None
            if self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
                return False, None
            # Verify reserved quantity matches cart quantity
            if (product_name not in self.reservations or
                user_id not in self.reservations[product_name] or
                self.reservations[product_name][user_id] != quantity):
                return False, None

        # Calculate total price
        total_price = sum(
            self.products[product_name].price * quantity
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
        )

        # Generate order ID
        order_id = f"{self.order_id_prefix}{str(self.next_order_id).zfill(self.order_id_digits)}"

        # Create order snapshot
        order_snapshot = {
            product_name: {
                'quantity': quantity,
                'price_at_checkout': self.products[product_name].price
            }
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
        }

        # Create and store order
        order = Order(
            order_id=order_id,
            user_id=user_id,
            total_price=total_price,
            products=order_snapshot
        )
        self.orders[order_id] = order

        # Clear cart and reservations
        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name]
            # Update product stock
            self.products[product_name].stock -= quantity
            self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity

        # Clear the cart
        self.users[user_id].cart.clear()

        # Increment order ID
        self.next_order_id += 1

    return True, order_id
===============================

def modify_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity, operation):

===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopBackend.perform_checkout: 
def perform_checkout(self, user_id):
    # Check if user exists
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False, None

    # Get user's cart
    cart = self.users[user_id].cart
    if not cart:
        return False, None

    # Acquire lock for thread-safe operations
    with self.lock:
        # Verify all products in cart are available with matching quantities
        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            if product_name not in self.products or product_name not in self.available_stock:
                return False, None
            if self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
                return False, None
            # Verify reserved quantity matches cart quantity
            if (product_name not in self.reservations or
                user_id not in self.reservations[product_name] or
                self.reservations[product_name][user_id] != quantity):
                return False, None
            # Verify reserved quantity matches available stock
            if self.reservations[product_name][user_id] != self.available_stock[product_name]:
                return False, None

        # Calculate total price
        total_price = sum(
            self.products[product_name].price * quantity
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
        )

        # Generate order ID
        order_id = f"{self.order_id_prefix}{str(self.next_order_id).zfill(self.order_id_digits)}"

        # Create order snapshot
        order_snapshot = {
            product_name: {
                'quantity': quantity,
                'price_at_checkout': self.products[product_name].price
            }
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
        }

        # Create and store order
        order = Order(
            order_id=order_id,
            user_id=user_id,
            total_price=total_price,
            products=order_snapshot
        )
        self.orders[order_id] = order

        # Clear cart and reservations
        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name]
            # Update product stock
            self.products[product_name].stock -= quantity
            self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity

        # Clear the cart
        self.users[user_id].cart.clear()

        # Increment order ID
        self.next_order_id += 1

    return True, order_id

def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> bool:
    # Check if the order exists
    if order_id not in self.orders:
        return False

    order = self.orders[order_id]
    current_status = order.status

    # Check if transition is from 'cancelled' (explicitly ignore)
    if current_status == 'cancelled':
        return False

    # Use Order's is_valid_transition method for validation
    if not order.is_valid_transition(new_status):
        return False

    # Handle cancellation separately
    if new_status == 'cancelled':
        with self.lock:
            for product_name, item_data in order.products.items():
                quantity = item_data['quantity']
                price_at_checkout = item_data['price_at_checkout']

                if product_name in self.products:
                    # Product exists, increase available stock
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                else:
                    # Product doesn't exist, recreate it with order snapshot details
                    self.products[product_name] = Product(product_name, price_at_checkout, quantity)
                    self.available_stock[product_name] = quantity

    # Update the order status
    order.status = new_status
    return True

Regenerated code for EShopBackend.get_system_state: 
def get_system_state(self):
    """Provides access to the current system state.

    Returns:
        dict: Dictionary containing the current system state with keys:
            - 'users': Current users with user IDs and their carts
            - 'products': Current products with names, prices, and available stock
            - 'available_stock': Products not reserved in any cart
            - 'reservations': All current reservations in the system
            - 'orders': All current orders and their statuses
    """
    system_state = {
        "users": {user_id: user.cart for user_id, user in self.users.items()},
        "products": {
            name: {
                "price": product.price,
                "stock": product.stock,
                "available_stock": self.available_stock.get(name, 0)
            } for name, product in self.products.items()
        },
        "available_stock": self.available_stock.copy(),
        "reservations": self.reservations.copy(),
        "orders": {order_id: order.status for order_id, order in self.orders.items()}
    }
    return system_state
