===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
import threading

class User:
    def __init__(self, user_id):
        self.user_id = user_id
        self.cart = {}

class Product:
    def __init__(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int):
        self.name = name
        self.price = price
        self.stock = stock

class Order:
    def __init__(self, order_id: str, user_id: str, products: dict, total_price: float):
        self.order_id = order_id
        self.user_id = user_id
        self.status = 'pending'
        self.products = products
        self.total_price = total_price
        self.valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
            'delivered': ['cancelled'],
            'cancelled': []
        }

    def is_valid_transition(self, new_status):
        """Checks if a status transition is valid."""
        if self.status in self.valid_transitions and new_status in self.valid_transitions[self.status]:
            return True
        return False

class EShopBackend:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}
        self.order_id_prefix = "SK"
        self.order_id_digits = 5
        self.available_stock = {}
        self.lock = threading.Lock()

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id in self.users:
            return False
        self.users[user_id] = User(user_id)
        return True

    def delete_user(self, user_id: str) -> bool:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            # Get user's reservations
            user_reservations = {}
            for product_name, user_reservation in self.reservations.items():
                if user_id in user_reservation:
                    user_reservations[product_name] = user_reservation[user_id]

            # Process each reservation
            for product_name, quantity in user_reservations.items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    # Return stock to available_stock if product exists
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                # If product doesn't exist, skip stock adjustment (as per specification)

            # Remove user from reservations
            for product_name in user_reservations.keys():
                if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                    if not self.reservations[product_name]:  # Clean up empty product reservations
                        del self.reservations[product_name]

            # Remove user from users
            del self.users[user_id]

        return True

    def add_product(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int) -> bool:
        if not isinstance(name, str) or not name.strip() or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            if name in self.products:
                product = self.products[name]
                product.price = price
                product.stock += stock
                self.available_stock[name] += stock
            else:
                product = Product(name, price, stock)
                self.products[name] = product
                self.available_stock[name] = stock

        return True

    def delete_product(self, name: str) -> bool:
        if name not in self.products:
            return False

        del self.products[name]
        del self.available_stock[name]
        return True

    def modify_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity, operation):
        if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        if operation == 'add':
            if quantity <= 0:
                return False

            with self.lock:
                if product_name not in self.available_stock or self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
                    return False

                if product_name not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                    self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] = quantity
                else:
                    self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] += quantity

                if product_name not in self.reservations:
                    self.reservations[product_name] = {}
                if user_id not in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    self.reservations[product_name][user_id] = 0
                self.reservations[product_name][user_id] += quantity

                self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity
                return True

        elif operation == 'remove':
            if quantity <= 0:
                return False

            with self.lock:
                if product_name not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                    return False

                if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] < quantity:
                    return False

                if product_name in self.products:
                    if product_name not in self.available_stock:
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = 0
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                else:
                    if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                        del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                        if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                            del self.reservations[product_name]

                self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] -= quantity
                if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] <= 0:
                    del self.users[user_id].cart[product_name]
                return True

        return False

    def perform_checkout(self, user_id):
        # Check if user exists
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False, None

        # Get user's cart
        cart = self.users[user_id].cart
        if not cart:
            return False, None

        # Acquire lock for thread-safe operations
        with self.lock:
            # Verify all products in cart are available with matching quantities
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
                if product_name not in self.products or product_name not in self.available_stock:
                    return False, None
                if self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
                    return False, None
                # Verify reserved quantity matches cart quantity
                if (product_name not in self.reservations or
                    user_id not in self.reservations[product_name] or
                    self.reservations[product_name][user_id] != quantity):
                    return False, None
                # Verify reserved quantity matches available stock
                if self.reservations[product_name][user_id] != self.available_stock[product_name]:
                    return False, None

            # Calculate total price
            total_price = sum(
                self.products[product_name].price * quantity
                for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
            )

            # Generate order ID
            order_id = f"{self.order_id_prefix}{str(self.next_order_id).zfill(self.order_id_digits)}"

            # Create order snapshot
            order_snapshot = {
                product_name: {
                    'quantity': quantity,
                    'price_at_checkout': self.products[product_name].price
                }
                for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
            }

            # Create and store order
            order = Order(
                order_id=order_id,
                user_id=user_id,
                total_price=total_price,
                products=order_snapshot
            )
            self.orders[order_id] = order

            # Clear cart and reservations
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
                if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                    if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                        del self.reservations[product_name]
                # Update product stock
                self.products[product_name].stock -= quantity
                self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity

            # Clear the cart
            self.users[user_id].cart.clear()

            # Increment order ID
            self.next_order_id += 1

        return True, order_id

    def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> bool:
        # Check if the order exists
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order.status

        # Check if transition is from 'cancelled' (explicitly ignore)
        if current_status == 'cancelled':
            return False

        # Use Order's is_valid_transition method for validation
        if not order.is_valid_transition(new_status):
            return False

        # Handle cancellation separately
        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            with self.lock:
                for product_name, item_data in order.products.items():
                    quantity = item_data['quantity']
                    price_at_checkout = item_data['price_at_checkout']

                    if product_name in self.products:
                        # Product exists, increase available stock
                        self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                    else:
                        # Product doesn't exist, recreate it with order snapshot details
                        self.products[product_name] = Product(product_name, price_at_checkout, quantity)
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = quantity

        # Update the order status
        order.status = new_status
        return True

    def get_system_state(self):
        """Provides access to the current system state.

        Returns:
            dict: Dictionary containing the current system state with keys:
                - 'users': Current users with user IDs and their carts
                - 'products': Current products with names, prices, and available stock
                - 'available_stock': Products not reserved in any cart
                - 'reservations': All current reservations in the system
                - 'orders': All current orders and their statuses
        """
        system_state = {
            "users": {user_id: user.cart for user_id, user in self.users.items()},
            "products": {
                name: {
                    "price": product.price,
                    "stock": product.stock,
                    "available_stock": self.available_stock.get(name, 0)
                } for name, product in self.products.items()
            },
            "available_stock": self.available_stock.copy(),
            "reservations": self.reservations.copy(),
            "orders": {order_id: order.status for order_id, order in self.orders.items()}
        }
        return system_state

def main():
    # Demonstration of the e-shop system
    shop = EShopBackend()

    # Create users
    shop.create_user("user1")
    shop.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    shop.add_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    shop.add_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)

    # Modify carts
    shop.modify_cart("user1", "Laptop", 2, "add")
    shop.modify_cart("user1", "Phone", 1, "add")
    shop.modify_cart("user2", "Phone", 3, "add")

    # Perform checkout
    success, order_id = shop.perform_checkout("user1")
    if success:
        print(f"Checkout successful! Order ID: {order_id}")
    else:
        print("Checkout failed.")

    # Update order status
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "completed")

    # Delete user
    shop.delete_user("user2")

    # Delete product
    shop.delete_product("Phone")

    # Get system state
    state = shop.get_system_state()
    print("\nCurrent System State:")
    print(state)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

Here's the structured Use Case derived from the provided program:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** Customer, Administrator

**Goal:**
- Customer: Purchase products by adding them to cart, checking out, and tracking order status
- Administrator: Manage users, products, and order statuses in the e-shop system

---

**System Responsibilities:**
1. **User Management**
   - Create and delete user accounts
   - Maintain user-specific shopping carts

2. **Product Management**
   - Add, update, and delete products with pricing and stock information
   - Track available stock and reservations

3. **Cart Management**
   - Allow users to add/remove products from their cart
   - Reserve stock for products in carts to prevent overselling

4. **Order Processing**
   - Process checkout to create orders from user carts
   - Generate unique order IDs
   - Track order status and allow valid status transitions
   - Handle order cancellations and restock products

5. **Stock Management**
   - Maintain accurate stock levels for products
   - Adjust stock during cart modifications, checkout, and cancellations

6. **System State Monitoring**
   - Provide current system state including users, products, reservations, and orders

---

**Preconditions:**
1. System is initialized with empty user, product, and order databases
2. Thread-safety mechanisms are active for concurrent operations

---

**Postconditions:**
1. **User Management**
   - New user is created with an empty cart, or existing user is deleted with all associated data removed
   - User reservations are cleared and stock is returned upon deletion

2. **Product Management**
   - Product is added/updated with correct price and stock, or deleted from the system
   - Available stock is adjusted accordingly

3. **Cart Management**
   - User cart reflects added/removed products
   - Stock is reserved for products in carts and unavailable for other users

4. **Order Processing**
   - Order is created with unique ID, user details, product snapshot, and total price
   - Order status is updated according to valid transitions
   - Stock is adjusted during checkout and cancellations

5. **Stock Management**
   - Product stock and available stock are accurately maintained

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

1. **User Registration**
   - Actor requests to create a new user account
   - System creates user with unique ID and empty cart

2. **Product Addition**
   - Actor adds a new product with name, price, and stock
   - System stores product and initializes available stock

3. **Cart Modification**
   - Actor adds product to cart with specified quantity
   - System verifies product availability and reserves stock
   - System updates user cart and available stock

4. **Checkout Process**
   - Actor requests checkout for their cart
   - System verifies all products are available with correct quantities
   - System calculates total price and generates unique order ID
   - System creates order with product snapshot and clears user cart
   - System updates product stock and removes reservations

5. **Order Status Update**
   - Actor updates order status (e.g., to "processing")
   - System validates status transition and updates order status

6. **Order Cancellation**
   - Actor cancels an order
   - System restocks products and updates order status to "cancelled"

7. **User Deletion**
   - Actor deletes a user account
   - System removes user, clears reservations, and returns stock

8. **Product Deletion**
   - Actor deletes a product
   - System removes product from inventory

9. **System State Inquiry**
   - Actor requests current system state
   - System provides users, products, reservations, and orders

---

**Extensions:**

1. **User Registration Failure**
   - *1a. User ID already exists*
     - System rejects creation and notifies actor

2. **Product Addition Failure**
   - *2a. Invalid product details (empty name, negative price/stock)*
     - System rejects addition and notifies actor
   - *2b. Product already exists*
     - System updates existing product with new price and stock

3. **Cart Modification Failure**
   - *3a. User or product does not exist*
     - System rejects modification
   - *3b. Insufficient stock*
     - System rejects addition and notifies actor
   - *3c. Invalid quantity (≤ 0)*
     - System rejects operation
   - *3d. Product not in cart (for removal)*
     - System rejects removal

4. **Checkout Failure**
   - *4a. User does not exist*
     - System rejects checkout
   - *4b. Cart is empty*
     - System rejects checkout
   - *4c. Product unavailable or insufficient stock*
     - System rejects checkout
   - *4d. Reservation mismatch*
     - System rejects checkout

5. **Order Status Update Failure**
   - *5a. Order does not exist*
     - System rejects update
   - *5b. Invalid status transition*
     - System rejects update
   - *5c. Order is already cancelled*
     - System ignores update

6. **User Deletion Failure**
   - *6a. User does not exist*
     - System rejects deletion

7. **Product Deletion Failure**
   - *7a. Product does not exist*
     - System rejects deletion

---

**Special Requirements:**
1. **Thread Safety**
   - System must handle concurrent operations safely (e.g., cart modifications, stock updates)

2. **Stock Consistency**
   - System must ensure stock levels and reservations are always consistent

3. **Order ID Format**
   - Order IDs must follow the format: prefix ("SK") + 5-digit number (e.g., SK00001)

4. **Status Transitions**
   - Order status transitions must follow predefined rules (e.g., "pending" → "processing" or "cancelled")

5. **Product Snapshot**
   - Orders must store product details (price, quantity) at checkout time to handle future product changes

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
import threading

class User:
    def __init__(self, user_id):
        self.user_id = user_id
        self.cart = {}

class Product:
    def __init__(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int):
        self.name = name
        self.price = price
        self.stock = stock

class Order:
    def __init__(self, order_id: str, user_id: str, products: dict, total_price: float):
        self.order_id = order_id
        self.user_id = user_id
        self.status = 'pending'
        self.products = products
        self.total_price = total_price
        self.valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
            'delivered': ['cancelled'],
            'cancelled': []
        }

    def is_valid_transition(self, new_status):
        """Checks if a status transition is valid."""
        if self.status in self.valid_transitions and new_status in self.valid_transitions[self.status]:
            return True
        return False

class EShopBackend:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}
        self.order_id_prefix = "SK"
        self.order_id_digits = 5
        self.available_stock = {}
        self.lock = threading.Lock()

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id in self.users:
            return False
        self.users[user_id] = User(user_id)
        return True

    def delete_user(self, user_id: str) -> bool:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            # Get user's reservations
            user_reservations = {}
            for product_name, user_reservation in self.reservations.items():
                if user_id in user_reservation:
                    user_reservations[product_name] = user_reservation[user_id]

            # Process each reservation
            for product_name, quantity in user_reservations.items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    # Return stock to available_stock if product exists
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                # If product doesn't exist, skip stock adjustment (as per specification)

            # Remove user from reservations
            for product_name in user_reservations.keys():
                if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                    if not self.reservations[product_name]:  # Clean up empty product reservations
                        del self.reservations[product_name]

            # Remove user from users
            del self.users[user_id]

        return True

    def add_product(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int) -> bool:
        if not isinstance(name, str) or not name.strip() or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            if name in self.products:
                product = self.products[name]
                product.price = price
                product.stock += stock
                self.available_stock[name] += stock
            else:
                product = Product(name, price, stock)
                self.products[name] = product
                self.available_stock[name] = stock

        return True

    def delete_product(self, name: str) -> bool:
        if name not in self.products:
            return False

        # Remove from products but keep reservations
        del self.products[name]
        return True

    def modify_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity, operation):
        if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        if operation == 'add':
            if quantity <= 0:
                return False

            with self.lock:
                if product_name not in self.available_stock or self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
                    return False

                if product_name not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                    self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] = quantity
                else:
                    self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] += quantity

                if product_name not in self.reservations:
                    self.reservations[product_name] = {}
                if user_id not in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    self.reservations[product_name][user_id] = 0
                self.reservations[product_name][user_id] += quantity

                self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity
                return True

        elif operation == 'remove':
            if quantity <= 0:
                return False

            with self.lock:
                if product_name not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                    return False

                if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] < quantity:
                    return False

                if product_name in self.products:
                    if product_name not in self.available_stock:
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = 0
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                else:
                    if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                        del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                        if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                            del self.reservations[product_name]

                self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] -= quantity
                if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] <= 0:
                    del self.users[user_id].cart[product_name]
                return True

        return False

    def perform_checkout(self, user_id):
        # Check if user exists
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False, None

        # Get user's cart
        cart = self.users[user_id].cart
        if not cart:
            return False, None

        # Acquire lock for thread-safe operations
        with self.lock:
            # Verify all products in cart are available with matching quantities
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
                if product_name not in self.products or product_name not in self.available_stock:
                    return False, None
                if self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
                    return False, None
                # Verify reserved quantity matches cart quantity
                if (product_name not in self.reservations or
                    user_id not in self.reservations[product_name] or
                    self.reservations[product_name][user_id] != quantity):
                    return False, None

            # Calculate total price
            total_price = sum(
                self.products[product_name].price * quantity
                for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
            )

            # Generate order ID
            order_id = f"{self.order_id_prefix}{str(self.next_order_id).zfill(self.order_id_digits)}"

            # Create order snapshot
            order_snapshot = {
                product_name: {
                    'quantity': quantity,
                    'price_at_checkout': self.products[product_name].price
                }
                for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
            }

            # Create and store order
            order = Order(
                order_id=order_id,
                user_id=user_id,
                total_price=total_price,
                products=order_snapshot
            )
            self.orders[order_id] = order

            # Clear cart and reservations
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
                if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                    if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                        del self.reservations[product_name]
                # Update product stock
                self.products[product_name].stock -= quantity
                self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity

            # Clear the cart
            self.users[user_id].cart.clear()

            # Increment order ID
            self.next_order_id += 1

        return True, order_id

    def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> bool:
        # Check if the order exists
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order.status

        # Check if transition is from 'cancelled' (explicitly ignore)
        if current_status == 'cancelled':
            return False

        # Use Order's is_valid_transition method for validation
        if not order.is_valid_transition(new_status):
            return False

        # Handle cancellation separately
        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            with self.lock:
                for product_name, item_data in order.products.items():
                    quantity = item_data['quantity']
                    price_at_checkout = item_data['price_at_checkout']

                    if product_name in self.products:
                        # Product exists, increase available stock
                        self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                    else:
                        # Product doesn't exist, recreate it with order snapshot details
                        self.products[product_name] = Product(product_name, price_at_checkout, quantity)
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = quantity

        # Update the order status
        order.status = new_status
        return True

    def get_system_state(self):
        """Provides access to the current system state.

        Returns:
            dict: Dictionary containing the current system state with keys:
                - 'users': Current users with user IDs and their carts
                - 'products': Current products with names, prices, and available stock
                - 'available_stock': Products not reserved in any cart
                - 'reservations': All current reservations in the system
                - 'orders': All current orders and their statuses
        """
        system_state = {
            "users": {user_id: user.cart for user_id, user in self.users.items()},
            "products": {
                name: {
                    "price": product.price,
                    "stock": product.stock,
                    "available_stock": self.available_stock.get(name, 0)
                } for name, product in self.products.items()
            },
            "available_stock": self.available_stock.copy(),
            "reservations": self.reservations.copy(),
            "orders": {
                order_id: {
                    'status': order.status,
                    'products': order.products,
                    'total_price': order.total_price
                } for order_id, order in self.orders.items()
            }
        }
        return system_state

def main():
    # Demonstration of the e-shop system
    shop = EShopBackend()

    # Create users
    shop.create_user("user1")
    shop.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    shop.add_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    shop.add_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)

    # Modify carts
    shop.modify_cart("user1", "Laptop", 2, "add")
    shop.modify_cart("user1", "Phone", 1, "add")
    shop.modify_cart("user2", "Phone", 3, "add")

    # Perform checkout
    success, order_id = shop.perform_checkout("user1")
    if success:
        print(f"Checkout successful! Order ID: {order_id}")
    else:
        print("Checkout failed.")

    # Update order status
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "completed")

    # Delete user
    shop.delete_user("user2")

    # Delete product
    shop.delete_product("Phone")

    # Get system state
    state = shop.get_system_state()
    print("\nCurrent System State:")
    print(state)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

Here's the structured Use Case derived from the provided program:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** Customer, Administrator

**Goal:**
- Customer: Purchase products, manage shopping cart, and track order status
- Administrator: Manage product inventory, user accounts, and order processing

---

**System Responsibilities:**
1. **User Management**
   - Create and delete user accounts
   - Maintain user shopping carts

2. **Product Management**
   - Add, update, and delete products
   - Track product stock and available quantities
   - Reserve products for users during cart operations

3. **Order Processing**
   - Process checkout to create orders
   - Update order status through valid transitions
   - Handle order cancellation and stock restoration

4. **Concurrency Control**
   - Ensure thread-safe operations for shared resources (stock, reservations, orders)

5. **State Monitoring**
   - Provide current system state including users, products, reservations, and orders

---

**Preconditions:**
1. System is initialized with empty user, product, and order repositories
2. Thread-safety mechanisms are active for concurrent operations

---

**Postconditions:**
1. **User Management**
   - New user account exists with empty cart (on creation)
   - User account and associated cart/reservations are removed (on deletion)

2. **Product Management**
   - New product exists with specified price and stock (on addition)
   - Product stock is updated (on addition/modification)
   - Product is removed from inventory but reservations persist (on deletion)

3. **Cart Operations**
   - Product quantities are reserved in user cart
   - Available stock is reduced by reserved quantities
   - Cart modifications are reflected in user cart and system reservations

4. **Checkout**
   - Order is created with unique ID and "pending" status
   - Cart is cleared and reservations are removed
   - Product stock is reduced by purchased quantities

5. **Order Status Update**
   - Order status is updated according to valid transition rules
   - Stock is restored for cancelled orders

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

1. **User Registration**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Creates new user account with provided user ID

2. **Product Addition**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:*
     - Validates product details (name, price ≥ 0, stock ≥ 0)
     - Adds new product or updates existing product stock

3. **Cart Management**
   - *Actor:* Customer
   - *System:*
     - Validates product availability and requested quantity
     - Adds/removes product to/from user cart
     - Updates available stock and reservations

4. **Checkout Process**
   - *Actor:* Customer
   - *System:*
     - Validates cart contents and product availability
     - Calculates total order price
     - Generates unique order ID
     - Creates order with "pending" status
     - Clears user cart and updates product stock

5. **Order Status Update**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:*
     - Validates status transition
     - Updates order status
     - Restores stock if order is cancelled

6. **User Deletion**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:*
     - Returns reserved stock to available inventory
     - Removes user from reservations and system

7. **Product Deletion**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Removes product from inventory while preserving reservations

8. **System State Monitoring**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Returns current state of users, products, reservations, and orders

---

**Extensions:**

**3a. Invalid Cart Operation**
- *Condition:* Product does not exist, quantity ≤ 0, or insufficient stock
- *System:* Rejects operation and maintains current state

**4a. Failed Checkout**
- *Condition:* User/cart does not exist, cart is empty, or products unavailable
- *System:* Rejects checkout and maintains current state

**5a. Invalid Status Transition**
- *Condition:* Transition not in valid state machine (e.g., "pending" → "delivered")
- *System:* Rejects status update and maintains current order status

**5b. Order Cancellation**
- *Condition:* Order status is updated to "cancelled"
- *System:*
  - Restores product stock (creates product if deleted)
  - Updates order status

**6a. User Deletion with Active Reservations**
- *Condition:* User has active product reservations
- *System:*
  - Returns reserved stock to available inventory
  - Removes user from reservations before deletion

---

**Special Requirements:**
1. **Concurrency Control**
   - System must handle simultaneous operations from multiple users without data corruption

2. **Order ID Format**
   - Order IDs must follow the format "SK" + 5-digit sequential number (e.g., SK00001)

3. **Stock Management**
   - Available stock must be tracked separately from total stock to account for reservations

4. **State Consistency**
   - System must maintain consistent state across users, products, reservations, and orders

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
import threading

class User:
    def __init__(self, user_id):
        self.user_id = user_id
        self.cart = {}

class Product:
    def __init__(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int):
        self.name = name
        self.price = price
        self.stock = stock

class Order:
    def __init__(self, order_id: str, user_id: str, products: dict, total_price: float):
        self.order_id = order_id
        self.user_id = user_id
        self.status = 'pending'
        self.products = products
        self.total_price = total_price
        self.valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
            'delivered': ['cancelled'],
            'cancelled': []
        }

    def is_valid_transition(self, new_status):
        """Checks if a status transition is valid."""
        if self.status in self.valid_transitions and new_status in self.valid_transitions[self.status]:
            return True
        return False

class EShopBackend:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}
        self.order_id_prefix = "SK"
        self.order_id_digits = 5
        self.available_stock = {}
        self.lock = threading.Lock()

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id in self.users:
            return False
        self.users[user_id] = User(user_id)
        return True

    def delete_user(self, user_id: str) -> bool:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            # Get user's reservations
            user_reservations = {}
            for product_name, user_reservation in self.reservations.items():
                if user_id in user_reservation:
                    user_reservations[product_name] = user_reservation[user_id]

            # Process each reservation
            for product_name, quantity in user_reservations.items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    # Return stock to available_stock if product exists
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                # If product doesn't exist, skip stock adjustment (as per specification)

            # Remove user from reservations
            for product_name in user_reservations.keys():
                if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                    if not self.reservations[product_name]:  # Clean up empty product reservations
                        del self.reservations[product_name]

            # Remove user from users
            del self.users[user_id]

        return True

    def add_product(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int) -> bool:
        if not isinstance(name, str) or not name.strip() or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            if name in self.products:
                product = self.products[name]
                product.price = price
                product.stock += stock
                self.available_stock[name] += stock
            else:
                product = Product(name, price, stock)
                self.products[name] = product
                self.available_stock[name] = stock

        return True

    def delete_product(self, name: str) -> bool:
        if name not in self.products:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            # Remove from products and available_stock
            del self.products[name]
            if name in self.available_stock:
                del self.available_stock[name]
        return True

    def modify_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity, operation):
        if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        if operation == 'add':
            if quantity <= 0:
                return False

            with self.lock:
                if product_name not in self.available_stock or self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
                    return False

                if product_name not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                    self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] = quantity
                else:
                    self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] += quantity

                if product_name not in self.reservations:
                    self.reservations[product_name] = {}
                if user_id not in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    self.reservations[product_name][user_id] = 0
                self.reservations[product_name][user_id] += quantity

                self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity
                return True

        elif operation == 'remove':
            if quantity <= 0:
                return False

            with self.lock:
                if product_name not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                    return False

                if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] < quantity:
                    return False

                if product_name in self.products:
                    if product_name not in self.available_stock:
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = 0
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                else:
                    if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                        del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                        if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                            del self.reservations[product_name]

                self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] -= quantity
                if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] <= 0:
                    del self.users[user_id].cart[product_name]
                return True

        return False

    def perform_checkout(self, user_id):
        # Check if user exists
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False, None

        # Get user's cart
        cart = self.users[user_id].cart
        if not cart:
            return False, None

        # Acquire lock for thread-safe operations
        with self.lock:
            # Verify all products in cart are available with matching quantities
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
                if product_name not in self.products or product_name not in self.available_stock:
                    return False, None
                if self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
                    return False, None
                # Verify reserved quantity matches cart quantity
                if (product_name not in self.reservations or
                    user_id not in self.reservations[product_name] or
                    self.reservations[product_name][user_id] != quantity):
                    return False, None

            # Calculate total price
            total_price = sum(
                self.products[product_name].price * quantity
                for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
            )

            # Generate order ID
            order_id = f"{self.order_id_prefix}{str(self.next_order_id).zfill(self.order_id_digits)}"

            # Create order snapshot
            order_snapshot = {
                product_name: {
                    'quantity': quantity,
                    'price_at_checkout': self.products[product_name].price
                }
                for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
            }

            # Create and store order
            order = Order(
                order_id=order_id,
                user_id=user_id,
                total_price=total_price,
                products=order_snapshot
            )
            self.orders[order_id] = order

            # Clear cart and reservations
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
                if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                    if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                        del self.reservations[product_name]
                # Update product stock
                self.products[product_name].stock -= quantity
                self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity

            # Clear the cart
            self.users[user_id].cart.clear()

            # Increment order ID
            self.next_order_id += 1

        return True, order_id

    def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> bool:
        # Check if the order exists
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order.status

        # Check if transition is from 'cancelled' (explicitly ignore)
        if current_status == 'cancelled':
            return False

        # Use Order's is_valid_transition method for validation
        if not order.is_valid_transition(new_status):
            return False

        # Handle cancellation separately
        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            with self.lock:
                for product_name, item_data in order.products.items():
                    quantity = item_data['quantity']
                    price_at_checkout = item_data['price_at_checkout']

                    if product_name in self.products:
                        # Product exists, increase available stock
                        self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                        self.products[product_name].stock += quantity
                    else:
                        # Product doesn't exist, recreate it with order snapshot details
                        self.products[product_name] = Product(product_name, price_at_checkout, quantity)
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = quantity

        # Update the order status
        order.status = new_status
        return True

    def get_system_state(self):
        """Provides access to the current system state.

        Returns:
            dict: Dictionary containing the current system state with keys:
                - 'users': Current users with user IDs and their carts
                - 'products': Current products with names, prices, and available stock
                - 'available_stock': Products not reserved in any cart
                - 'reservations': All current reservations in the system
                - 'orders': All current orders and their statuses
        """
        system_state = {
            "users": {user_id: user.cart for user_id, user in self.users.items()},
            "products": {
                name: {
                    "price": product.price,
                    "stock": product.stock,
                    "available_stock": self.available_stock.get(name, 0)
                } for name, product in self.products.items()
            },
            "available_stock": self.available_stock.copy(),
            "reservations": self.reservations.copy(),
            "orders": {
                order_id: {
                    'status': order.status,
                    'products': order.products,
                    'total_price': order.total_price
                } for order_id, order in self.orders.items()
            }
        }
        return system_state

def main():
    # Demonstration of the e-shop system
    shop = EShopBackend()

    # Create users
    shop.create_user("user1")
    shop.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    shop.add_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    shop.add_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)

    # Modify carts
    shop.modify_cart("user1", "Laptop", 2, "add")
    shop.modify_cart("user1", "Phone", 1, "add")
    shop.modify_cart("user2", "Phone", 3, "add")

    # Perform checkout
    success, order_id = shop.perform_checkout("user1")
    if success:
        print(f"Checkout successful! Order ID: {order_id}")
    else:
        print("Checkout failed.")

    # Update order status
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "completed")

    # Delete user
    shop.delete_user("user2")

    # Delete product
    shop.delete_product("Phone")

    # Get system state
    state = shop.get_system_state()
    print("\nCurrent System State:")
    print(state)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== USE_CASE_EXTRACTION_FAILED OUTPUT =====
**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** Customer, Administrator

**Goal:**
- **Customer:** To browse products, manage a shopping cart, place orders, and track order status.
- **Administrator:** To manage product inventory, monitor system state, and handle user accounts.

---

### **System Responsibilities:**
1. **User Management:**
   - Create and delete user accounts.
   - Maintain user-specific shopping carts.

2. **Product Management:**
   - Add, update, and delete products in the inventory.
   - Track product stock levels and available quantities.
   - Reserve products for users during cart operations.

3. **Shopping Cart Management:**
   - Allow users to add or remove products from their cart.
   - Reserve product quantities in the cart to prevent overselling.
   - Release reserved stock if products are removed from the cart.

4. **Order Processing:**
   - Process checkout to create orders from user carts.
   - Generate unique order IDs for tracking.
   - Update product stock and available quantities upon order creation.
   - Support order status transitions (e.g., pending → processing → completed → shipped → delivered).
   - Handle order cancellations and restock products if an order is cancelled.

5. **State Monitoring:**
   - Provide a snapshot of the current system state, including users, products, reservations, and orders.

---

### **Preconditions:**
1. The system is initialized and operational.
2. For user-specific actions (e.g., cart operations, checkout), the user must exist in the system.
3. For product-specific actions (e.g., add to cart, checkout), the product must exist in the inventory.
4. For order status updates, the order must exist in the system.

---

### **Postconditions:**
1. **User Management:**
   - If a user is created, they are added to the system with an empty cart.
   - If a user is deleted, their cart and reservations are cleared, and reserved stock is released.

2. **Product Management:**
   - If a product is added or updated, its stock and available quantities are adjusted.
   - If a product is deleted, it is removed from the inventory, and its stock is no longer tracked.

3. **Shopping Cart Management:**
   - If a product is added to a cart, its quantity is reserved, and available stock is reduced.
   - If a product is removed from a cart, its reserved quantity is released, and available stock is increased.

4. **Order Processing:**
   - If checkout is successful, an order is created, the user’s cart is cleared, and product stock is updated.
   - If an order status is updated, the order’s state reflects the new status.
   - If an order is cancelled, reserved stock is restocked, and product quantities are adjusted.

5. **State Monitoring:**
   - The system state snapshot reflects the latest state of users, products, reservations, and orders.

---

### **Main Success Scenario:**

#### **1. User Management**
1. **Create User:**
   - Actor requests to create a new user with a unique ID.
   - System validates the user ID does not already exist.
   - System creates the user with an empty cart.
   - System confirms successful creation.

2. **Delete User:**
   - Actor requests to delete a user by ID.
   - System validates the user exists.
   - System releases all reserved stock for the user’s cart.
   - System removes the user from the system.
   - System confirms successful deletion.

---

#### **2. Product Management**
1. **Add/Update Product:**
   - Actor requests to add a new product or update an existing product with name, price, and stock.
   - System validates the product details (e.g., non-negative price and stock).
   - System adds the product to the inventory or updates its stock and price.
   - System confirms successful addition/update.

2. **Delete Product:**
   - Actor requests to delete a product by name.
   - System validates the product exists.
   - System removes the product from the inventory.
   - System confirms successful deletion.

---

#### **3. Shopping Cart Management**
1. **Add Product to Cart:**
   - Actor requests to add a product to their cart with a specified quantity.
   - System validates the user and product exist and the quantity is available.
   - System reserves the product quantity and reduces available stock.
   - System updates the user’s cart.
   - System confirms successful addition.

2. **Remove Product from Cart:**
   - Actor requests to remove a product from their cart with a specified quantity.
   - System validates the product exists in the user’s cart.
   - System releases the reserved quantity and increases available stock.
   - System updates the user’s cart.
   - System confirms successful removal.

---

#### **4. Order Processing**
1. **Perform Checkout:**
   - Actor requests to checkout their cart.
   - System validates the user exists and the cart is not empty.
   - System verifies all products in the cart are available with sufficient stock.
   - System calculates the total order price.
   - System generates a unique order ID.
   - System creates an order with the cart contents and sets its status to "pending."
   - System clears the user’s cart and releases reservations.
   - System updates product stock and available quantities.
   - System confirms successful checkout and provides the order ID.

2. **Update Order Status:**
   - Actor requests to update an order’s status (e.g., to "processing" or "cancelled").
   - System validates the order exists and the status transition is valid.
   - If the new status is "cancelled," system restocks the products in the order.
   - System updates the order status.
   - System confirms successful status update.

---

#### **5. State Monitoring**
1. **Get System State:**
   - Actor requests the current system state.
   - System compiles a snapshot of users, products, reservations, and orders.
   - System returns the system state to the actor.

---

### **Extensions:**

#### **1. User Management**
- **User Already Exists (Create User):**
  - System detects the user ID already exists.
  - System notifies the actor that the user cannot be created.

- **User Does Not Exist (Delete User):**
  - System detects the user does not exist.
  - System notifies the actor that the user cannot be deleted.

---

#### **2. Product Management**
- **Invalid Product Details (Add/Update Product):**
  - System detects invalid product details (e.g., negative price or stock).
  - System notifies the actor that the product cannot be added/updated.

- **Product Does Not Exist (Delete Product):**
  - System detects the product does not exist.
  - System notifies the actor that the product cannot be deleted.

---

#### **3. Shopping Cart Management**
- **User or Product Does Not Exist (Add/Remove Product):**
  - System detects the user or product does not exist.
  - System notifies the actor that the operation cannot be completed.

- **Insufficient Stock (Add Product):**
  - System detects insufficient available stock for the requested quantity.
  - System notifies the actor that the product cannot be added to the cart.

- **Product Not in Cart (Remove Product):**
  - System detects the product is not in the user’s cart.
  - System notifies the actor that the product cannot be removed.

---

#### **4. Order Processing**
- **Empty Cart (Perform Checkout):**
  - System detects the user’s cart is empty.
  - System notifies the actor that checkout cannot be completed.

- **Insufficient Stock (Perform Checkout):**
  - System detects insufficient stock for one or more products in the cart.
  - System notifies the actor that checkout cannot be completed.

- **Invalid Status Transition (Update Order Status):**
  - System detects the requested status transition is invalid.
  - System notifies the actor that the status cannot be updated.

- **Order Does Not Exist (Update Order Status):**
  - System detects the order does not exist.
  - System notifies the actor that the status cannot be updated.

- **Order Already Cancelled (Update Order Status):**
  - System detects the order is already cancelled.
  - System notifies the actor that no further status updates are allowed.

---

### **Special Requirements:**
1. **Thread Safety:**
   - The system must ensure thread-safe operations for concurrent user interactions (e.g., cart modifications, checkout, order status updates).

2. **Stock Reservation:**
   - The system must reserve product quantities when added to a cart to prevent overselling.

3. **Order ID Generation:**
   - The system must generate unique order IDs with a consistent format (e.g., "SK00001").

4. **State Consistency:**
   - The system must maintain consistent state across users, products, reservations, and orders at all times.

      "message": "In `update_order_status`, the method does not enforce the rule that transitions from 'cancelled' must be explicitly ignored. While the method checks for `current_status == 'cancelled'`, it does not explicitly ignore transitions from 'cancelled' as a terminal state (e.g., no warning or strict enforcement)."
    },
    {
      "type": "llm_semantic",
      "message": "In `modify_cart`, the method does not handle the case where a product no longer exists during a 'remove' operation correctly. The reservation is removed, but the cart entry is not cleaned up if the product no longer exists (only the reservation is removed)."
    },
    {
      "type": "llm_semantic",
      "message": "In `delete_product`, the method does not preserve reservations for the deleted product as required by the Use Case. Reservations should remain tracked but no longer affect available stock, but the method does not explicitly ensure this."
    },
    {
      "type": "llm_semantic",
      "message": "In `add_product`, the method does not validate that the product name is non-empty (only checks `not name.strip()`). While this is minor, it could lead to inconsistent product names if empty strings are passed."
    }
  ]
]

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
import threading

class User:
    def __init__(self, user_id):
        self.user_id = user_id
        self.cart = {}

class Product:
    def __init__(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int):
        self.name = name
        self.price = price
        self.stock = stock

class Order:
    def __init__(self, order_id: str, user_id: str, products: dict, total_price: float):
        self.order_id = order_id
        self.user_id = user_id
        self.status = 'pending'
        self.products = products
        self.total_price = total_price
        self.valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
            'delivered': ['cancelled'],
            'cancelled': []
        }

    def is_valid_transition(self, new_status):
        """Checks if a status transition is valid."""
        if self.status in self.valid_transitions and new_status in self.valid_transitions[self.status]:
            return True
        return False

class EShopBackend:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}
        self.order_id_prefix = "SK"
        self.order_id_digits = 5
        self.available_stock = {}
        self.lock = threading.Lock()

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id in self.users:
            return False
        self.users[user_id] = User(user_id)
        return True

    def delete_user(self, user_id: str) -> bool:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            # Get user's reservations
            user_reservations = {}
            for product_name, user_reservation in self.reservations.items():
                if user_id in user_reservation:
                    user_reservations[product_name] = user_reservation[user_id]

            # Process each reservation
            for product_name, quantity in user_reservations.items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    # Return stock to available_stock if product exists
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                # If product doesn't exist, skip stock adjustment (as per specification)

            # Remove user from reservations
            for product_name in user_reservations.keys():
                if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                    if not self.reservations[product_name]:  # Clean up empty product reservations
                        del self.reservations[product_name]

            # Remove user from users
            del self.users[user_id]

        return True

    def add_product(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int) -> bool:
        if not isinstance(name, str) or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            if name in self.products:
                product = self.products[name]
                product.price = price
                product.stock += stock
                self.available_stock[name] += stock
            else:
                product = Product(name, price, stock)
                self.products[name] = product
                self.available_stock[name] = stock

        return True

    def delete_product(self, name: str) -> bool:
        if name not in self.products:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            # Remove from products and available_stock
            del self.products[name]
            if name in self.available_stock:
                del self.available_stock[name]
        return True

    def modify_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity, operation):
        if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        if operation == 'add':
            if quantity <= 0:
                return False

            with self.lock:
                if product_name not in self.available_stock or self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
                    return False

                if product_name not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                    self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] = quantity
                else:
                    self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] += quantity

                if product_name not in self.reservations:
                    self.reservations[product_name] = {}
                if user_id not in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    self.reservations[product_name][user_id] = 0
                self.reservations[product_name][user_id] += quantity

                self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity
                return True

        elif operation == 'remove':
            if quantity <= 0:
                return False

            with self.lock:
                if product_name not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                    return False

                if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] < quantity:
                    return False

                if product_name in self.products:
                    if product_name not in self.available_stock:
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = 0
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                else:
                    if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                        del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                        if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                            del self.reservations[product_name]

                self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] -= quantity
                if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] <= 0:
                    del self.users[user_id].cart[product_name]
                return True

        return False

    def perform_checkout(self, user_id):
        # Check if user exists
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False, None

        # Get user's cart
        cart = self.users[user_id].cart
        if not cart:
            return False, None

        # Acquire lock for thread-safe operations
        with self.lock:
            # Verify all products in cart are available with matching quantities
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
                if product_name not in self.products or product_name not in self.available_stock:
                    return False, None
                if self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
                    return False, None
                # Verify reserved quantity matches cart quantity
                if (product_name not in self.reservations or
                    user_id not in self.reservations[product_name] or
                    self.reservations[product_name][user_id] != quantity):
                    return False, None

            # Calculate total price
            total_price = sum(
                self.products[product_name].price * quantity
                for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
            )

            # Generate order ID
            order_id = f"{self.order_id_prefix}{str(self.next_order_id).zfill(self.order_id_digits)}"

            # Create order snapshot
            order_snapshot = {
                product_name: {
                    'quantity': quantity,
                    'price_at_checkout': self.products[product_name].price
                }
                for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
            }

            # Create and store order
            order = Order(
                order_id=order_id,
                user_id=user_id,
                total_price=total_price,
                products=order_snapshot
            )
            self.orders[order_id] = order

            # Clear cart and reservations
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
                if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                    if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                        del self.reservations[product_name]
                # Update product stock
                self.products[product_name].stock -= quantity
                self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity

            # Clear the cart
            self.users[user_id].cart.clear()

            # Increment order ID
            self.next_order_id += 1

        return True, order_id

    def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> bool:
        # Check if the order exists
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order.status

        # Check if transition is from 'cancelled' (explicitly ignore)
        if current_status == 'cancelled':
            return False

        # Use Order's is_valid_transition method for validation
        if not order.is_valid_transition(new_status):
            return False

        # Handle cancellation separately
        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            with self.lock:
                for product_name, item_data in order.products.items():
                    quantity = item_data['quantity']
                    price_at_checkout = item_data['price_at_checkout']

                    if product_name in self.products:
                        # Product exists, increase available stock
                        self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                        self.products[product_name].stock += quantity
                    else:
                        # Product doesn't exist, recreate it with order snapshot details
                        self.products[product_name] = Product(product_name, price_at_checkout, quantity)
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = quantity

        # Update the order status
        order.status = new_status
        return True

    def get_system_state(self):
        """Provides access to the current system state.

        Returns:
            dict: Dictionary containing the current system state with keys:
                - 'users': Current users with user IDs and their carts
                - 'products': Current products with names, prices, and available stock
                - 'available_stock': Products not reserved in any cart
                - 'reservations': All current reservations in the system
                - 'orders': All current orders and their statuses
        """
        system_state = {
            "users": {user_id: user.cart for user_id, user in self.users.items()},
            "products": {
                name: {
                    "price": product.price,
                    "stock": product.stock,
                    "available_stock": self.available_stock.get(name, 0)
                } for name, product in self.products.items()
            },
            "available_stock": self.available_stock.copy(),
            "reservations": self.reservations.copy(),
            "orders": {
                order_id: {
                    'status': order.status,
                    'products': order.products,
                    'total_price': order.total_price
                } for order_id, order in self.orders.items()
            }
        }
        return system_state

def main():
    # Demonstration of the e-shop system
    shop = EShopBackend()

    # Create users
    shop.create_user("user1")
    shop.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    shop.add_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    shop.add_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)

    # Modify carts
    shop.modify_cart("user1", "Laptop", 2, "add")
    shop.modify_cart("user1", "Phone", 1, "add")
    shop.modify_cart("user2", "Phone", 3, "add")

    # Perform checkout
    success, order_id = shop.perform_checkout("user1")
    if success:
        print(f"Checkout successful! Order ID: {order_id}")
    else:
        print("Checkout failed.")

    # Update order status
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "completed")

    # Delete user
    shop.delete_user("user2")

    # Delete product
    shop.delete_product("Phone")

    # Get system state
    state = shop.get_system_state()
    print("\nCurrent System State:")
    print(state)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

def test_main_success_scenario():
    shop = EShopBackend()
    # Create user
    assert shop.create_user("user1") is True
    # Add product
    assert shop.add_product("laptop", 999.99, 10) is True
    # Modify cart
    assert shop.modify_cart("user1", "laptop", 2, "add") is True
    # Perform checkout
    success, order_id = shop.perform_checkout("user1")
    assert success is True
    assert order_id == "SK00001"
    # Update order status
    assert shop.update_order_status(order_id, "processing") is True

def test_user_deletion_with_reservations():
    shop = EShopBackend()
    # Create user and add product
    shop.create_user("user2")
    shop.add_product("phone", 599.99, 5)
    # Modify cart and checkout
    shop.modify_cart("user2", "phone", 3, "add")
    success, order_id = shop.perform_checkout("user2")
    # Delete user
    assert shop.delete_user("user2") is True
    # Verify user is deleted and stock is updated
    state = shop.get_system_state()
    assert "user2" not in state["users"]
    assert state["available_stock"]["phone"] == 5  # 5 original + 3 returned

def test_product_deletion_with_existing_reservations():
    shop = EShopBackend()
    # Create user and add product
    shop.create_user("user3")
    shop.add_product("tablet", 299.99, 8)
    # Modify cart but don't checkout
    shop.modify_cart("user3", "tablet", 4, "add")
    # Delete product
    assert shop.delete_product("tablet") is True
    # Verify product is deleted but reservation remains
    state = shop.get_system_state()
    assert "tablet" not in state["products"]
    assert state["reservations"]["tablet"]["user3"] == 4

  :ignore request;
endif
:process "user1" checkout;
if (cart not empty?) then (yes)
  :verify all reserved products available;
  if (all products available?) then (yes)
    :calculate total price;
    :generate unique order ID;
    :create order with pending status;
    :clear "user1" cart and reservations;
    :update product stock;
    :increment next order ID;
    :confirm checkout success;
  else (no)
    :fail checkout;
  endif
else (no)
  :ignore checkout request;
endif
:update order status to "processing";
:update order status to "completed";
:delete user "user2";
if (user exists?) then (yes)
  :cancel all "user2" reservations;
  if (product exists?) then (yes)
    :return reserved quantity to stock;
  else (no)
    :remove reservation without stock adjustment;
  endif
  :remove user from system;
else (no)
  :ignore request;
endif
:delete product "Phone";
if (product exists?) then (yes)
  :remove product from available stock;
else (no)
  :ignore request;
endif
:get current system state;
:display system state;
stop
@enduml
===============================


===== OUTPUT_review_activity_diagram OUTPUT =====
Here are the identified defects in the PlantUML activity diagram:

- **Missing atomic checkout failure handling**: The diagram does not show the rollback behavior when checkout fails (e.g., when reserved products are no longer available). The program explicitly fails checkout and applies no changes, but the diagram only shows a simple "fail checkout" without indicating that no state changes occur.

- **Incorrect order status transition flow**: The diagram shows a direct transition from "pending" to "completed" (`:update order status to "completed"`), bypassing the required "processing" state. The program enforces valid transitions via `is_valid_transition()`, which does not allow `pending → completed`.

- **Missing order status transition validation**: The diagram does not reflect the program's strict transition rules (e.g., no transitions from "cancelled" are allowed). The `update_order_status()` method explicitly checks these rules, but the diagram shows unconditional status updates.

- **Incorrect user deletion behavior**: The diagram shows `:remove user from system` before canceling reservations, but the program cancels all reservations *before* removing the user (inside the `delete_user()` method). The flow order is reversed.

- **Missing product recreation during cancellation**: When an order is cancelled, the program recreates deleted products with their original price/quantity (if they no longer exist). The diagram omits this step in the cancellation flow (only shows stock adjustment for existing products).

- **Incorrect stock adjustment during product deletion**: The diagram shows `:remove product from available stock` during product deletion, but the program preserves reservations and orders for the deleted product (reservations remain tracked but no longer affect available stock). The diagram implies stock is fully removed, which contradicts the program's behavior.

- **Missing cart emptiness check in checkout**: The diagram shows `:verify all reserved products available` before checking if the cart is empty, but the program checks `if not cart` *before* any verification (atomicity requirement). The flow order is incorrect.
===============================
