@enduml
===============================


===== user_feedback OUTPUT =====
The system has incorrect stock handling during checkout.

Reserved quantities are already deducted from available stock when items are added to the cart. However, during checkout the system deducts stock again, which leads to incorrect stock values.

Checkout should not reduce stock again. It should only finalize existing reservations.

Additionally, checkout validation is incorrect. It should rely on reservations, not on available stock, since available stock already excludes reserved items.

Because of this, stock becomes inconsistent and order cancellation does not fully restore it.

Fix:

- Do not deduct stock during checkout
- Validate checkout using reservations
- Ensure stock is correctly restored on cancellation
===============================


===== OUTPUT_generate_use_case OUTPUT =====
Here's the revised Use Case with the required changes applied:

---

Use Case: Manage E-Shop Backend System
Primary Actor: User (Administrator/Customer)
Goal: Manage users, products, carts, reservations, and orders in an e-shop backend system while ensuring correct handling of current available stock.

---

**System Responsibilities:**
- Manage user accounts, including creation and deletion.
- Maintain product catalog with attributes (name, price, stock).
- Handle cart operations (add/remove products) and reservations.
- Process checkout to create orders with atomicity.
- Update order statuses following strict transition rules.
- Ensure stock consistency during reservations, checkouts, and cancellations.
- Generate globally unique and sequential order IDs.
- Provide access to current system state (users, products, stock, carts, orders).

---

**Preconditions:**
- The e-shop backend system is initialized and operational.
- No prior state corruption exists (e.g., stock inconsistencies).

---

**Postconditions:**
- Users, products, carts, reservations, and orders are in a consistent state.
- Stock levels reflect all successful operations (reservations, checkouts, cancellations).
- Order statuses adhere to the defined transition rules.
- Order IDs are unique and sequential.

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

### 1. Create User
1. Actor requests to create a user with a unique ID.
2. System validates the ID is not a duplicate.
3. System creates the user and an empty cart.
4. System confirms user creation.

### 2. Delete User
1. Actor requests to delete a user by ID.
2. System validates the user exists.
3. System removes the user's cart and cancels all their reservations:
   - For each reserved product:
     - If the product exists in stock, the reserved quantity is returned to available stock.
     - If the product no longer exists, the reservation is removed without stock adjustment.
4. System deletes the user while preserving their orders (orders remain unaffected and accessible in the system).
5. System confirms user deletion.

### 3. Add Product
1. Actor requests to add a product with name, price, and stock (zero stock allowed).
2. System validates the input (e.g., non-negative stock).
3. If the product name already exists:
   - System increases the current available stock by the provided quantity.
   - System updates the product price to the provided price.
4. If the product name does not exist:
   - System creates a new product with the provided attributes.
5. System confirms product addition/update.

### 4. Delete Product
1. Actor requests to delete a product by name.
2. System validates the product exists.
3. System removes the product from the current available stock system while preserving all existing reservations and orders for the product (reservations remain tracked but no longer affect available stock; reserved quantities are still deducted from the deleted product's stock record).
4. System confirms product deletion.

### 5. Modify Cart
1. Actor requests to add/remove a product in their cart with a specified quantity.
2. System validates the user and product exist.
3. **Add Product to Cart:**
   - If the requested quantity is zero, system ignores the request.
   - System checks if the requested quantity is available in stock.
   - If the product is already in the cart, system updates the reserved quantity (increases by the specified amount).
   - If the product is not in the cart, system reserves the quantity (deducts from available stock, making it unavailable to other users) and adds it to the cart.
   - If unavailable, system ignores the request.
4. **Remove Product from Cart:**
   - System checks if the removal quantity is valid (≤ reserved amount).
   - If valid, system reduces/removes the reservation:
     - If the product exists in stock, the quantity is returned to available stock.
     - If the product no longer exists, the reservation is removed without stock adjustment.
   - If invalid, system ignores the request.
5. System confirms cart modification.

### 6. Perform Checkout
1. Actor requests to checkout their cart.
2. System validates the cart is not empty.
3. System verifies all reserved products still exist in the system and that the reserved quantities match the cart contents. If any reserved product no longer exists or the reserved quantity is inconsistent with the cart, system fails checkout and applies no changes.
4. System calculates the total price using current product prices.
5. System creates a new order with:
   - Status "pending".
   - A snapshot of product prices at checkout.
   - A globally unique and sequential ID (e.g., "SK00001").
6. System clears the cart and corresponding reservations without modifying stock levels.
7. System confirms checkout success.

### 7. Update Order Status
1. Actor requests to update an order's status.
2. System validates the order exists and the new status is valid.
3. System checks the transition follows the allowed sequence:
   - `pending → processing → completed → shipped → delivered`
   - Any status → `cancelled` (except from `cancelled`; no transitions from `cancelled` are allowed).
   - From `delivered`, only → `cancelled` (no other transitions allowed).
4. If transitioning to `cancelled`:
   - For each product in the order:
     - If the product exists in stock, system increases available stock by the ordered quantity.
     - If the product no longer exists, system recreates it with the price and quantity stored in the order.
5. System updates the order status.
6. System confirms status update.

---

**Extensions:**

### 1a. Create User
1a. Duplicate user ID → System ignores the request.

### 2a. Delete User
2a. User does not exist → System ignores the request.

### 3a. Add Product
3a. Invalid input (e.g., negative stock) → System ignores the request.

### 4a. Delete Product
4a. Product does not exist → System ignores the request.

### 5a. Modify Cart
5a. User or product does not exist → System ignores the request.
5b. Add product with zero quantity → System ignores the request.
5c. Add product with insufficient stock → System ignores the request.
5d. Remove product with invalid quantity → System ignores the request.

### 6a. Perform Checkout
6a. Cart is empty → System ignores the request.
6b. Reserved product no longer exists or reserved quantity inconsistent with cart → System fails checkout and applies no changes.
6c. Any checkout failure → System rolls back all changes (atomicity).

### 7a. Update Order Status
7a. Order does not exist → System ignores the request.
7b. Invalid status transition → System ignores the request.
7c. Transition from `cancelled` → System ignores the request.

---

**Special Requirements:**
- All operations must maintain system consistency (e.g., stock levels, reservations).
- Order IDs must be globally unique, sequential, and never reused.
- Invalid inputs (e.g., negative quantities) must be ignored without side effects.
- The system must handle concurrent operations safely (implied by atomicity requirements).
- Reserved quantities must not be available to other users during the reservation period.
- The implementation must use object-oriented design in Python.
===============================

===== OUTPUT_review_architecture OUTPUT =====
{
  "score": 0.95,
  "issues": [
    "The `perform_checkout` method's description and steps in the Current Architecture correctly reflect the feedback to rely solely on reservation data and not modify stock levels during checkout, but the 'special requirements' section still mentions 'Stock levels must remain unchanged during checkout (no further deduction or restoration occurs)' which is redundant since stock modification is already excluded from the steps. This could cause confusion about whether stock is ever modified during checkout.",
    "The `perform_checkout` method's 'special requirements' section does not explicitly state that checkout validation must rely solely on reservation data (ignoring available stock), which is a key requirement from the feedback and Updated Use Case. This should be added for clarity and completeness.",
    "The `update_order_status` method's description and steps correctly handle stock restoration on cancellation, but the 'special requirements' section does not explicitly mention that products must be recreated with the exact price and quantity from the order snapshot if they no longer exist. This is a critical requirement from the feedback and should be explicitly stated."
  ]
}

===== generate_or_update_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopBackend.perform_checkout: 
def perform_checkout(self, user_id):
    # Check if user exists
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False, None

    # Get user's cart
    cart = self.users[user_id].cart
    if not cart:
        return False, None

    # Acquire lock for thread-safe operations
    with self.lock:
        # Verify all products in cart are available with matching quantities
        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            if product_name not in self.products:
                return False, None
            # Verify reserved quantity matches cart quantity
            if (product_name not in self.reservations or
                user_id not in self.reservations[product_name] or
                self.reservations[product_name][user_id] != quantity):
                return False, None

        # Calculate total price
        total_price = sum(
            self.products[product_name].price * quantity
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
        )

        # Generate order ID
        order_id = f"{self.order_id_prefix}{str(self.next_order_id).zfill(self.order_id_digits)}"

        # Create order snapshot
        order_snapshot = {
            product_name: {
                'quantity': quantity,
                'price_at_checkout': self.products[product_name].price
            }
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
        }

        # Create and store order
        order = Order(
            order_id=order_id,
            user_id=user_id,
            total_price=total_price,
            products=order_snapshot
        )
        self.orders[order_id] = order

        # Clear cart and reservations
        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name]

        # Clear the cart
        self.users[user_id].cart.clear()

        # Increment order ID
        self.next_order_id += 1

    return True, order_id

===== generate_or_update_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopBackend.update_order_status: 
def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> bool:
    # Check if the order exists
    if order_id not in self.orders:
        return False

    order = self.orders[order_id]
    current_status = order.status

    # Check if transition is from 'cancelled' (explicitly ignore)
    if current_status == 'cancelled':
        return False

    # Use Order's is_valid_transition method for validation
    if not order.is_valid_transition(new_status):
        return False

    # Handle cancellation separately
    if new_status == 'cancelled':
        with self.lock:
            for product_name, item_data in order.products.items():
                quantity = item_data['quantity']
                price_at_checkout = item_data['price_at_checkout']

                if product_name in self.products:
                    # Product exists, increase available stock
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                else:
                    # Product doesn't exist, recreate it with order snapshot details
                    self.products[product_name] = Product(product_name, price_at_checkout, quantity)
                    self.available_stock[product_name] = quantity

    # Update the order status
    order.status = new_status
    return True

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
import threading

class User:
    def __init__(self, user_id):
        self.user_id = user_id
        self.cart = {}

class Product:
    def __init__(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int):
        self.name = name
        self.price = price
        self.stock = stock

class Order:
    def __init__(self, order_id: str, user_id: str, products: dict, total_price: float):
        self.order_id = order_id
        self.user_id = user_id
        self.status = 'pending'
        self.products = products
        self.total_price = total_price
        self.valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
            'delivered': ['cancelled'],
            'cancelled': []
        }

    def is_valid_transition(self, new_status):
        """Checks if a status transition is valid."""
        if self.status in self.valid_transitions and new_status in self.valid_transitions[self.status]:
            return True
        return False

class EShopBackend:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}
        self.order_id_prefix = "SK"
        self.order_id_digits = 5
        self.available_stock = {}
        self.lock = threading.Lock()

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id in self.users:
            return False
        self.users[user_id] = User(user_id)
        return True

    def delete_user(self, user_id: str) -> bool:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            # Get user's reservations
            user_reservations = {}
            for product_name, user_reservation in self.reservations.items():
                if user_id in user_reservation:
                    user_reservations[product_name] = user_reservation[user_id]

            # Process each reservation
            for product_name, quantity in user_reservations.items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    # Return stock to available_stock if product exists
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                # If product doesn't exist, skip stock adjustment (as per specification)

            # Remove user from reservations
            for product_name in user_reservations.keys():
                if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                    if not self.reservations[product_name]:  # Clean up empty product reservations
                        del self.reservations[product_name]

            # Remove user from users
            del self.users[user_id]

        return True

    def add_product(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int) -> bool:
        if not isinstance(name, str) or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            if name in self.products:
                product = self.products[name]
                product.price = price
                product.stock += stock
                self.available_stock[name] += stock
            else:
                product = Product(name, price, stock)
                self.products[name] = product
                self.available_stock[name] = stock

        return True

    def delete_product(self, name: str) -> bool:
        if name not in self.products:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            # Remove from products and available_stock
            del self.products[name]
            if name in self.available_stock:
                del self.available_stock[name]
        return True

    def modify_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity, operation):
        if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        if operation == 'add':
            if quantity <= 0:
                return False

            with self.lock:
                if product_name not in self.available_stock or self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
                    return False

                if product_name not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                    self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] = quantity
                else:
                    self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] += quantity

                if product_name not in self.reservations:
                    self.reservations[product_name] = {}
                if user_id not in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    self.reservations[product_name][user_id] = 0
                self.reservations[product_name][user_id] += quantity

                self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity
                return True

        elif operation == 'remove':
            if quantity <= 0:
                return False

            with self.lock:
                if product_name not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                    return False

                if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] < quantity:
                    return False

                if product_name in self.products:
                    if product_name not in self.available_stock:
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = 0
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                else:
                    if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                        del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                        if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                            del self.reservations[product_name]

                self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] -= quantity
                if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] <= 0:
                    del self.users[user_id].cart[product_name]
                return True

        return False

    def perform_checkout(self, user_id):
        # Check if user exists
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False, None

        # Get user's cart
        cart = self.users[user_id].cart
        if not cart:
            return False, None

        # Acquire lock for thread-safe operations
        with self.lock:
            # Verify all products in cart are available with matching quantities
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
                if product_name not in self.products:
                    return False, None
                if (product_name not in self.reservations or
                    user_id not in self.reservations[product_name] or
                    self.reservations[product_name][user_id] != quantity):
                    return False, None

            # Calculate total price
            total_price = sum(
                self.products[product_name].price * quantity
                for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
            )

            # Generate order ID
            order_id = f"{self.order_id_prefix}{str(self.next_order_id).zfill(self.order_id_digits)}"

            # Create order snapshot
            order_snapshot = {
                product_name: {
                    'quantity': quantity,
                    'price_at_checkout': self.products[product_name].price
                }
                for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
            }

            # Create and store order
            order = Order(
                order_id=order_id,
                user_id=user_id,
                total_price=total_price,
                products=order_snapshot
            )
            self.orders[order_id] = order

            # Clear cart and reservations
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
                if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                    if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                        del self.reservations[product_name]

            # Clear the cart
            self.users[user_id].cart.clear()

            # Increment order ID
            self.next_order_id += 1

        return True, order_id

    def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> bool:
        # Check if the order exists
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order.status

        # Check if transition is from 'cancelled' (explicitly ignore)
        if current_status == 'cancelled':
            return False

        # Use Order's is_valid_transition method for validation
        if not order.is_valid_transition(new_status):
            return False

        # Handle cancellation separately
        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            with self.lock:
                for product_name, item_data in order.products.items():
                    quantity = item_data['quantity']
                    price_at_checkout = item_data['price_at_checkout']

                    if product_name in self.products:
                        # Product exists, increase available stock
                        self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                        self.products[product_name].stock += quantity
                    else:
                        # Product doesn't exist, recreate it with order snapshot details
                        self.products[product_name] = Product(product_name, price_at_checkout, quantity)
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = quantity

        # Update the order status
        order.status = new_status
        return True

    def get_system_state(self):
        """Provides access to the current system state.

        Returns:
            dict: Dictionary containing the current system state with keys:
                - 'users': Current users with user IDs and their carts
                - 'products': Current products with names, prices, and available stock
                - 'available_stock': Products not reserved in any cart
                - 'reservations': All current reservations in the system
                - 'orders': All current orders and their statuses
        """
        system_state = {
            "users": {user_id: user.cart for user_id, user in self.users.items()},
            "products": {
                name: {
                    "price": product.price,
                    "stock": product.stock,
                    "available_stock": self.available_stock.get(name, 0)
                } for name, product in self.products.items()
            },
            "available_stock": self.available_stock.copy(),
            "reservations": self.reservations.copy(),
            "orders": {
                order_id: {
                    'status': order.status,
                    'products': order.products,
                    'total_price': order.total_price
                } for order_id, order in self.orders.items()
            }
        }
        return system_state

def main():
    # Demonstration of the e-shop system
    shop = EShopBackend()

    # Create users
    shop.create_user("user1")
    shop.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    shop.add_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    shop.add_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)

    # Modify carts
    shop.modify_cart("user1", "Laptop", 2, "add")
    shop.modify_cart("user1", "Phone", 1, "add")
    shop.modify_cart("user2", "Phone", 3, "add")

    # Perform checkout
    success, order_id = shop.perform_checkout("user1")
    if success:
        print(f"Checkout successful! Order ID: {order_id}")
    else:
        print("Checkout failed.")

    # Update order status
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "completed")

    # Delete user
    shop.delete_user("user2")

    # Delete product
    shop.delete_product("Phone")

    # Get system state
    state = shop.get_system_state()
    print("\nCurrent System State:")
    print(state)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

Here's the structured Use Case derived from the provided program:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** Customer, Administrator

**Goal:**
- Customer: Purchase products, manage shopping cart, and track order status
- Administrator: Manage product inventory, monitor system state, and handle user accounts

---

**System Responsibilities:**
1. **User Management**
   - Create and delete user accounts
   - Maintain user-specific shopping carts

2. **Product Management**
   - Add, update, and delete products in the inventory
   - Track product stock levels and available quantities
   - Reserve products for users during cart operations

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Add and remove products from user carts
   - Reserve product quantities when added to cart
   - Release reservations when products are removed or orders are cancelled

4. **Order Processing**
   - Process checkout to create orders from user carts
   - Generate unique order identifiers
   - Track order status and allow valid status transitions
   - Handle order cancellation and restock products

5. **Concurrency Control**
   - Ensure thread-safe operations for shared resources (stock, reservations, orders)

6. **System State Monitoring**
   - Provide current system state including users, products, reservations, and orders

---

**Preconditions:**
1. System is initialized with empty user, product, and order repositories
2. Thread-safety mechanisms are active for concurrent operations

---

**Postconditions:**
1. **User Management**
   - New user account exists with empty cart (on creation)
   - User account and associated data are removed (on deletion)

2. **Product Management**
   - Product exists in inventory with specified price and stock (on addition)
   - Product is removed from inventory (on deletion)
   - Product stock is updated when modified

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - User cart contains specified products with correct quantities
   - Product reservations reflect cart contents
   - Available stock is adjusted based on cart operations

4. **Order Processing**
   - Order is created with unique ID, user reference, product snapshot, and total price
   - Order status is updated according to valid transitions
   - Products are restocked when orders are cancelled
   - User cart is cleared after successful checkout

5. **System State**
   - System state reflects all current users, products, reservations, and orders

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

1. **User Registration**
   - Actor requests to create a new user account with a unique ID
   - System creates the user account and initializes an empty shopping cart

2. **Product Addition**
   - Actor adds a new product with name, price, and stock quantity
   - System adds the product to inventory with available stock matching total stock

3. **Cart Management**
   - Actor adds a product to their cart with specified quantity
   - System verifies product availability and reserves the quantity
   - System updates the user's cart and adjusts available stock

4. **Checkout Process**
   - Actor requests to checkout their current cart
   - System verifies all products in cart are available and reserved for the user
   - System calculates total price and generates a unique order ID
   - System creates an order with product snapshot and clears the user's cart
   - System releases product reservations

5. **Order Status Update**
   - Actor updates the order status to a valid next state (e.g., "processing")
   - System validates the status transition and updates the order status

6. **System State Monitoring**
   - Actor requests the current system state
   - System provides a snapshot of users, products, reservations, and orders

---

**Extensions:**

**1a. User Registration Failure**
   - User ID already exists
   - System rejects the request and notifies the actor

**2a. Product Addition Failure**
   - Invalid product details (negative price/stock or non-string name)
   - System rejects the request and notifies the actor

**3a. Cart Modification Failure**
   - User or product does not exist
   - Insufficient stock available
   - Invalid quantity specified
   - System rejects the request and notifies the actor

**4a. Checkout Failure**
   - User does not exist
   - Cart is empty
   - Products in cart are not available or not reserved for the user
   - System rejects the request and notifies the actor

**5a. Order Status Update Failure**
   - Order does not exist
   - Invalid status transition requested
   - Order is already cancelled
   - System rejects the request and notifies the actor

**6a. Product Deletion**
   - Actor requests to delete a product
   - System removes the product from inventory and available stock

**7a. User Deletion**
   - Actor requests to delete a user
   - System releases all product reservations held by the user
   - System removes the user account and associated data

**8a. Order Cancellation**
   - Actor cancels an order
   - System restocks products from the order (if products still exist)
   - System recreates products with order snapshot details if they no longer exist
   - System updates the order status to "cancelled"

---

**Special Requirements:**
1. **Concurrency Control**
   - The system must handle concurrent operations safely to prevent race conditions on shared resources (e.g., stock, reservations).

2. **Order ID Format**
   - Order IDs must follow the format: prefix ("SK") followed by a 5-digit number, zero-padded.

3. **Stock Management**
   - Available stock must be accurately tracked and adjusted during cart operations, checkout, and order cancellation.

4. **Status Transitions**
   - Order status transitions must follow predefined valid paths (e.g., "pending" → "processing" → "completed" → "shipped" → "delivered").

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
import threading

class User:
    def __init__(self, user_id):
        self.user_id = user_id
        self.cart = {}

class Product:
    def __init__(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int):
        self.name = name
        self.price = price
        self.stock = stock

class Order:
    def __init__(self, order_id: str, user_id: str, products: dict, total_price: float):
        self.order_id = order_id
        self.user_id = user_id
        self.status = 'pending'
        self.products = products
        self.total_price = total_price
        self.valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
            'delivered': ['cancelled'],
            'cancelled': []
        }

    def is_valid_transition(self, new_status):
        """Checks if a status transition is valid."""
        if self.status in self.valid_transitions and new_status in self.valid_transitions[self.status]:
            return True
        return False

class EShopBackend:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}
        self.order_id_prefix = "SK"
        self.order_id_digits = 5
        self.available_stock = {}
        self.lock = threading.Lock()

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id in self.users:
            return False
        self.users[user_id] = User(user_id)
        return True

    def delete_user(self, user_id: str) -> bool:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            # Get user's reservations
            user_reservations = {}
            for product_name, user_reservation in self.reservations.items():
                if user_id in user_reservation:
                    user_reservations[product_name] = user_reservation[user_id]

            # Process each reservation
            for product_name, quantity in user_reservations.items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    # Return stock to available_stock if product exists
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                # If product doesn't exist, skip stock adjustment (as per specification)

            # Remove user from reservations
            for product_name in user_reservations.keys():
                if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                    if not self.reservations[product_name]:  # Clean up empty product reservations
                        del self.reservations[product_name]

            # Remove user from users
            del self.users[user_id]

        return True

    def add_product(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int) -> bool:
        if not isinstance(name, str) or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            if name in self.products:
                product = self.products[name]
                product.price = price
                product.stock += stock
                self.available_stock[name] += stock
            else:
                product = Product(name, price, stock)
                self.products[name] = product
                self.available_stock[name] = stock

        return True

    def delete_product(self, name: str) -> bool:
        if name not in self.products:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            # Remove from products and available_stock
            del self.products[name]
            if name in self.available_stock:
                del self.available_stock[name]
        return True

    def modify_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity, operation):
        if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        if operation == 'add':
            if quantity <= 0:
                return False

            with self.lock:
                if product_name not in self.available_stock or self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
                    return False

                if product_name not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                    self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] = quantity
                else:
                    self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] += quantity

                if product_name not in self.reservations:
                    self.reservations[product_name] = {}
                if user_id not in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    self.reservations[product_name][user_id] = 0
                self.reservations[product_name][user_id] += quantity

                self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity
                return True

        elif operation == 'remove':
            if quantity <= 0:
                return False

            with self.lock:
                if product_name not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                    return False

                if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] < quantity:
                    return False

                if product_name in self.products:
                    if product_name not in self.available_stock:
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = 0
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                else:
                    if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                        del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                        if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                            del self.reservations[product_name]

                self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] -= quantity
                if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] <= 0:
                    del self.users[user_id].cart[product_name]
                return True

        return False

    def perform_checkout(self, user_id):
        # Check if user exists
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False, None

        # Get user's cart
        cart = self.users[user_id].cart
        if not cart:
            return False, None

        # Acquire lock for thread-safe operations
        with self.lock:
            # Verify all products in cart are available with matching quantities
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
                if product_name not in self.reservations or user_id not in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    return False, None
                if self.reservations[product_name][user_id] != quantity:
                    return False, None

            # Calculate total price
            total_price = sum(
                self.products[product_name].price * quantity
                for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
            )

            # Generate order ID
            order_id = f"{self.order_id_prefix}{str(self.next_order_id).zfill(self.order_id_digits)}"

            # Create order snapshot
            order_snapshot = {
                product_name: {
                    'quantity': quantity,
                    'price_at_checkout': self.products[product_name].price
                }
                for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
            }

            # Create and store order
            order = Order(
                order_id=order_id,
                user_id=user_id,
                total_price=total_price,
                products=order_snapshot
            )
            self.orders[order_id] = order

            # Clear cart and reservations
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
                if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                    if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                        del self.reservations[product_name]

            # Clear the cart
            self.users[user_id].cart.clear()

            # Increment order ID
            self.next_order_id += 1

        return True, order_id

    def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> bool:
        # Check if the order exists
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order.status

        # Check if transition is from 'cancelled' (explicitly ignore)
        if current_status == 'cancelled':
            return False

        # Use Order's is_valid_transition method for validation
        if not order.is_valid_transition(new_status):
            return False

        # Handle cancellation separately
        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            with self.lock:
                for product_name, item_data in order.products.items():
                    quantity = item_data['quantity']
                    price_at_checkout = item_data['price_at_checkout']

                    if product_name in self.products:
                        # Product exists, increase available stock
                        self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                        self.products[product_name].stock += quantity
                    else:
                        # Product doesn't exist, recreate it with order snapshot details
                        self.products[product_name] = Product(product_name, price_at_checkout, quantity)
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = quantity

        # Update the order status
        order.status = new_status
        return True

    def get_system_state(self):
        """Provides access to the current system state.

        Returns:
            dict: Dictionary containing the current system state with keys:
                - 'users': Current users with user IDs and their carts
                - 'products': Current products with names, prices, and available stock
                - 'available_stock': Products not reserved in any cart
                - 'reservations': All current reservations in the system
                - 'orders': All current orders and their statuses
        """
        system_state = {
            "users": {user_id: user.cart for user_id, user in self.users.items()},
            "products": {
                name: {
                    "price": product.price,
                    "stock": product.stock,
                    "available_stock": self.available_stock.get(name, 0)
                } for name, product in self.products.items()
            },
            "available_stock": self.available_stock.copy(),
            "reservations": self.reservations.copy(),
            "orders": {
                order_id: {
                    'status': order.status,
                    'products': order.products,
                    'total_price': order.total_price
                } for order_id, order in self.orders.items()
            }
        }
        return system_state

def main():
    # Demonstration of the e-shop system
    shop = EShopBackend()

    # Create users
    shop.create_user("user1")
    shop.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    shop.add_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    shop.add_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)

    # Modify carts
    shop.modify_cart("user1", "Laptop", 2, "add")
    shop.modify_cart("user1", "Phone", 1, "add")
    shop.modify_cart("user2", "Phone", 3, "add")

    # Perform checkout
    success, order_id = shop.perform_checkout("user1")
    if success:
        print(f"Checkout successful! Order ID: {order_id}")
    else:
        print("Checkout failed.")

    # Update order status
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "completed")

    # Delete user
    shop.delete_user("user2")

    # Delete product
    shop.delete_product("Phone")

    # Get system state
    state = shop.get_system_state()
    print("\nCurrent System State:")
    print(state)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** Customer, Administrator

**Goal:**
- Customer: Purchase products, manage shopping cart, and track order status
- Administrator: Manage product inventory, monitor system state, and handle user accounts

**System Responsibilities:**
1. Manage user accounts (creation, deletion)
2. Maintain product inventory (add, update, delete products)
3. Handle shopping cart operations (add/remove items, stock reservation)
4. Process checkouts and generate orders
5. Manage order lifecycle (status transitions, cancellations)
6. Maintain thread-safe operations for concurrent access
7. Provide system state visibility for monitoring

**Preconditions:**
1. System is initialized with empty user, product, and order databases
2. Thread safety mechanisms are active
3. Unique ID generation is configured (order IDs)

**Postconditions:**
1. User accounts are created/deleted with associated carts cleared
2. Product inventory reflects current stock levels and availability
3. Orders are created with unique IDs and proper status tracking
4. Shopping carts are empty after successful checkout
5. Stock reservations are properly released for cancelled orders
6. System state is consistent across all operations

**Main Success Scenario:**

1. Administrator adds new products to inventory
2. Customer creates user account
3. Customer browses products and adds items to cart
4. System reserves stock for cart items
5. Customer initiates checkout
6. System:
   a. Validates cart contents and stock availability
   b. Calculates total price
   c. Generates unique order ID
   d. Creates order with "pending" status
   e. Clears customer's cart
7. Administrator updates order status through valid transitions
8. System maintains order history and updates stock accordingly

**Extensions:**

1a. Product addition fails:
   - If product data is invalid (negative price/stock)
   - If product already exists (updates existing product)

2a. User creation fails:
   - If user ID already exists

3a. Cart modification fails:
   - If user doesn't exist
   - If product doesn't exist
   - If requested quantity exceeds available stock
   - If invalid operation specified

5a. Checkout fails:
   - If user doesn't exist
   - If cart is empty
   - If stock reservations don't match cart contents

7a. Order status update fails:
   - If order doesn't exist
   - If transition is invalid
   - If order is already cancelled

8a. User deletion:
   - System releases all stock reservations for the user
   - System removes user from all reservations

8b. Product deletion:
   - System removes product from inventory
   - System removes product from available stock tracking

**Special Requirements:**
1. System must handle concurrent operations safely (thread-safe)
2. Order IDs must follow specific format (prefix + sequential number)
3. Stock availability must be accurately tracked in real-time
4. Order status transitions must follow predefined workflow
5. System must maintain historical product prices for completed orders

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
import threading

class User:
    def __init__(self, user_id):
        self.user_id = user_id
        self.cart = {}

class Product:
    def __init__(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int):
        self.name = name
        self.price = price
        self.stock = stock

class Order:
    def __init__(self, order_id: str, user_id: str, products: dict, total_price: float):
        self.order_id = order_id
        self.user_id = user_id
        self.status = 'pending'
        self.products = products
        self.total_price = total_price
        self.valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
            'delivered': ['cancelled'],
            'cancelled': []
        }

    def is_valid_transition(self, new_status):
        """Checks if a status transition is valid."""
        if self.status in self.valid_transitions and new_status in self.valid_transitions[self.status]:
            return True
        return False

class EShopBackend:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}
        self.order_id_prefix = "SK"
        self.order_id_digits = 5
        self.available_stock = {}
        self.lock = threading.Lock()

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id in self.users:
            return False
        self.users[user_id] = User(user_id)
        return True

    def delete_user(self, user_id: str) -> bool:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            # Get user's reservations
            user_reservations = {}
            for product_name, user_reservation in self.reservations.items():
                if user_id in user_reservation:
                    user_reservations[product_name] = user_reservation[user_id]

            # Process each reservation
            for product_name, quantity in user_reservations.items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    # Return stock to available_stock if product exists
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                # If product doesn't exist, skip stock adjustment (as per specification)

            # Remove user from reservations
            for product_name in user_reservations.keys():
                if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                    if not self.reservations[product_name]:  # Clean up empty product reservations
                        del self.reservations[product_name]

            # Remove user from users
            del self.users[user_id]

        return True

    def add_product(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int) -> bool:
        if not isinstance(name, str) or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            if name in self.products:
                product = self.products[name]
                product.price = price
                product.stock += stock
                self.available_stock[name] += stock
            else:
                product = Product(name, price, stock)
                self.products[name] = product
                self.available_stock[name] = stock

        return True

    def delete_product(self, name: str) -> bool:
        if name not in self.products:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            # Remove from products and available_stock
            del self.products[name]
            if name in self.available_stock:
                del self.available_stock[name]
            # Preserve reservations for the deleted product
            # (as per Use Case requirement)
        return True

    def modify_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity, operation):
        if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        if operation == 'add':
            if quantity <= 0:
                return False

            with self.lock:
                if product_name not in self.available_stock or self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
                    return False

                if product_name not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                    self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] = quantity
                else:
                    self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] += quantity

                if product_name not in self.reservations:
                    self.reservations[product_name] = {}
                if user_id not in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    self.reservations[product_name][user_id] = 0
                self.reservations[product_name][user_id] += quantity

                self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity
                return True

        elif operation == 'remove':
            if quantity <= 0:
                return False

            with self.lock:
                if product_name not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                    return False

                if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] < quantity:
                    return False

                if product_name in self.products:
                    if product_name not in self.available_stock:
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = 0
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                else:
                    if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                        del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                        if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                            del self.reservations[product_name]

                self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] -= quantity
                if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] <= 0:
                    del self.users[user_id].cart[product_name]
                return True

        return False

    def perform_checkout(self, user_id):
        # Check if user exists
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False, None

        # Get user's cart
        cart = self.users[user_id].cart
        if not cart:
            return False, None

        # Acquire lock for thread-safe operations
        with self.lock:
            # Verify all products in cart are available with matching quantities
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
                if product_name not in self.reservations or user_id not in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    return False, None
                if self.reservations[product_name][user_id] != quantity:
                    return False, None

            # Calculate total price
            total_price = sum(
                self.products[product_name].price * quantity
                for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
            )

            # Generate order ID
            order_id = f"{self.order_id_prefix}{str(self.next_order_id).zfill(self.order_id_digits)}"

            # Create order snapshot
            order_snapshot = {
                product_name: {
                    'quantity': quantity,
                    'price_at_checkout': self.products[product_name].price
                }
                for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
            }

            # Create and store order
            order = Order(
                order_id=order_id,
                user_id=user_id,
                total_price=total_price,
                products=order_snapshot
            )
            self.orders[order_id] = order

            # Clear cart and reservations
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
                if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                    if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                        del self.reservations[product_name]

            # Clear the cart
            self.users[user_id].cart.clear()

            # Increment order ID
            self.next_order_id += 1

        return True, order_id

    def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> bool:
        # Check if the order exists
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order.status

        # Check if transition is from 'cancelled' (explicitly ignore)
        if current_status == 'cancelled':
            return False

        # Use Order's is_valid_transition method for validation
        if not order.is_valid_transition(new_status):
            return False

        # Handle cancellation separately
        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            with self.lock:
                for product_name, item_data in order.products.items():
                    quantity = item_data['quantity']
                    price_at_checkout = item_data['price_at_checkout']

                    if product_name in self.products:
                        # Product exists, increase available stock
                        self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                        self.products[product_name].stock += quantity
                    else:
                        # Product doesn't exist, recreate it with order snapshot details
                        self.products[product_name] = Product(product_name, price_at_checkout, quantity)
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = quantity

        # Update the order status
        order.status = new_status
        return True

    def get_system_state(self):
        """Provides access to the current system state.

        Returns:
            dict: Dictionary containing the current system state with keys:
                - 'users': Current users with user IDs and their carts
                - 'products': Current products with names, prices, and available stock
                - 'available_stock': Products not reserved in any cart
                - 'reservations': All current reservations in the system
                - 'orders': All current orders and their statuses
                - 'next_order_id': The next available order ID
        """
        system_state = {
            "users": {user_id: user.cart for user_id, user in self.users.items()},
            "products": {
                name: {
                    "price": product.price,
                    "stock": product.stock,
                    "available_stock": self.available_stock.get(name, 0)
                } for name, product in self.products.items()
            },
            "available_stock": self.available_stock.copy(),
            "reservations": self.reservations.copy(),
            "orders": {
                order_id: {
                    'status': order.status,
                    'products': order.products,
                    'total_price': order.total_price
                } for order_id, order in self.orders.items()
            },
            "next_order_id": self.next_order_id
        }
        return system_state

def main():
    # Demonstration of the e-shop system
    shop = EShopBackend()

    # Create users
    shop.create_user("user1")
    shop.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    shop.add_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    shop.add_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)

    # Modify carts
    shop.modify_cart("user1", "Laptop", 2, "add")
    shop.modify_cart("user1", "Phone", 1, "add")
    shop.modify_cart("user2", "Phone", 3, "add")

    # Perform checkout
    success, order_id = shop.perform_checkout("user1")
    if success:
        print(f"Checkout successful! Order ID: {order_id}")
    else:
        print("Checkout failed.")

    # Update order status
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "completed")

    # Delete user
    shop.delete_user("user2")

    # Delete product
    shop.delete_product("Phone")

    # Get system state
    state = shop.get_system_state()
    print("\nCurrent System State:")
    print(state)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

Here's the structured Use Case derived from the provided program:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** Customer, Administrator

**Goal:**
- Customer: Purchase products, manage shopping cart, and track order status
- Administrator: Manage product inventory, monitor system state, and handle user accounts

---

**System Responsibilities:**
1. **User Management**
   - Create and delete user accounts
   - Maintain user-specific shopping carts

2. **Product Management**
   - Add, update, and delete products in the inventory
   - Track product stock levels and available quantities
   - Reserve products for users during cart operations

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Add and remove products from user carts
   - Reserve product quantities when added to cart
   - Release reservations when products are removed or orders are cancelled

4. **Order Processing**
   - Process user checkouts to create orders
   - Generate unique order identifiers
   - Track order status through defined transitions
   - Handle order cancellations and restock products

5. **Stock Management**
   - Maintain available stock separate from total product stock
   - Adjust stock levels during cart operations, checkouts, and cancellations
   - Handle stock for deleted products

6. **System State Monitoring**
   - Provide current system state including users, products, reservations, and orders

---

**Preconditions:**
1. System is initialized with empty user, product, and order databases
2. Thread-safety mechanisms are active for concurrent operations

---

**Postconditions:**
1. **User Management**
   - New user account exists with empty cart (on creation)
   - User account and all associated data are removed (on deletion)

2. **Product Management**
   - Product exists in inventory with updated price and stock (on add/update)
   - Product is removed from inventory but reservations are preserved (on deletion)

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Product quantities are reserved in user cart
   - Available stock is reduced by cart quantities
   - Reservations are released when products are removed

4. **Order Processing**
   - Order is created with unique ID and "pending" status
   - Cart is cleared and reservations are released (on successful checkout)
   - Order status is updated according to valid transitions
   - Products are restocked when orders are cancelled

5. **Stock Management**
   - Available stock reflects current reservations
   - Total product stock is updated during cancellations

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

1. **User Registration**
   - Actor requests to create a new user account
   - System creates user account with unique ID and empty cart

2. **Product Addition**
   - Actor adds a new product with name, price, and stock quantity
   - System adds product to inventory with specified details

3. **Cart Management**
   - Actor adds product to cart with specified quantity
   - System verifies product availability
   - System reserves product quantity and reduces available stock
   - System updates user cart with product and quantity

4. **Checkout Process**
   - Actor requests to checkout current cart
   - System verifies all cart products are available and reserved
   - System calculates total order price
   - System generates unique order ID
   - System creates order with "pending" status
   - System clears user cart and releases reservations

5. **Order Status Update**
   - Actor updates order status to next valid state
   - System verifies status transition validity
   - System updates order status

6. **Order Cancellation**
   - Actor cancels an order
   - System verifies order is not already cancelled
   - System restocks products from cancelled order
   - System updates order status to "cancelled"

7. **User Deletion**
   - Actor deletes a user account
   - System releases all product reservations for the user
   - System returns reserved stock to available stock
   - System removes user account and cart

8. **Product Deletion**
   - Actor deletes a product
   - System removes product from inventory
   - System preserves existing reservations for the product

9. **System State Monitoring**
   - Actor requests current system state
   - System provides comprehensive state including users, products, reservations, and orders

---

**Extensions:**

1. **User Registration Failure**
   - *1a. User ID already exists*
     - System rejects creation and notifies actor

2. **Product Addition Failure**
   - *2a. Invalid product details (negative price/stock)*
     - System rejects addition and notifies actor
   - *2b. Product already exists*
     - System updates existing product with new price and stock

3. **Cart Management Failure**
   - *3a. User or product does not exist*
     - System rejects operation
   - *3b. Insufficient stock available*
     - System rejects addition and notifies actor
   - *3c. Invalid quantity (≤ 0)*
     - System rejects operation
   - *3d. Product not in cart (for removal)*
     - System rejects removal
   - *3e. Removal quantity exceeds cart quantity*
     - System rejects removal

4. **Checkout Failure**
   - *4a. User does not exist*
     - System rejects checkout
   - *4b. Cart is empty*
     - System rejects checkout
   - *4c. Product reservations do not match cart*
     - System rejects checkout

5. **Order Status Update Failure**
   - *5a. Order does not exist*
     - System rejects status update
   - *5b. Invalid status transition*
     - System rejects status update
   - *5c. Order is already cancelled*
     - System rejects status update

6. **User Deletion Failure**
   - *6a. User does not exist*
     - System rejects deletion

7. **Product Deletion Failure**
   - *7a. Product does not exist*
     - System rejects deletion

---

**Special Requirements:**
1. **Concurrency Control**
   - System must handle concurrent operations safely using locking mechanisms

2. **Order ID Format**
   - Order IDs must follow the format: prefix ("SK") + 5-digit sequential number

3. **Stock Management**
   - System must maintain separate available stock that reflects current reservations
   - Deleted products must preserve their reservations for existing orders

4. **Status Transitions**
   - Order status transitions must follow predefined valid paths:
     - pending → processing | cancelled
     - processing → completed | cancelled
     - completed → shipped | cancelled
     - shipped → delivered | cancelled
     - delivered → cancelled
     - cancelled → (no further transitions)

5. **Data Preservation**
   - When cancelling orders for deleted products, system must recreate product with original price and quantity

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
import threading

class User:
    def __init__(self, user_id):
        self.user_id = user_id
        self.cart = {}

class Product:
    def __init__(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int):
        self.name = name
        self.price = price
        self.stock = stock

class Order:
    def __init__(self, order_id: str, user_id: str, products: dict, total_price: float):
        self.order_id = order_id
        self.user_id = user_id
        self.status = 'pending'
        self.products = products
        self.total_price = total_price
        self.valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
            'delivered': ['cancelled'],
            'cancelled': []
        }

    def is_valid_transition(self, new_status):
        """Checks if a status transition is valid."""
        if self.status in self.valid_transitions and new_status in self.valid_transitions[self.status]:
            return True
        return False

class EShopBackend:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}
        self.order_id_prefix = "SK"
        self.order_id_digits = 5
        self.available_stock = {}
        self.lock = threading.Lock()

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id in self.users:
            return False
        self.users[user_id] = User(user_id)
        return True

    def delete_user(self, user_id: str) -> bool:
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            # Get user's reservations
            user_reservations = {}
            for product_name, user_reservation in self.reservations.items():
                if user_id in user_reservation:
                    user_reservations[product_name] = user_reservation[user_id]

            # Process each reservation
            for product_name, quantity in user_reservations.items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    # Return stock to available_stock if product exists
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                # If product doesn't exist, skip stock adjustment (as per specification)

            # Remove user from reservations
            for product_name in user_reservations.keys():
                if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                    if not self.reservations[product_name]:  # Clean up empty product reservations
                        del self.reservations[product_name]

            # Remove user from users
            del self.users[user_id]

        return True

    def add_product(self, name: str, price: float, stock: int) -> bool:
        if not isinstance(name, str) or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            if name in self.products:
                product = self.products[name]
                product.price = price
                product.stock += stock
                self.available_stock[name] += stock
            else:
                product = Product(name, price, stock)
                self.products[name] = product
                self.available_stock[name] = stock

        return True

    def delete_product(self, name: str) -> bool:
        if name not in self.products:
            return False

        with self.lock:
            # Remove from products and available_stock
            del self.products[name]
            if name in self.available_stock:
                del self.available_stock[name]
            # Preserve reservations for the deleted product
            # (as per Use Case requirement)
        return True

    def modify_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity, operation):
        if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        if operation == 'add':
            if quantity <= 0:
                return False

            with self.lock:
                if product_name not in self.available_stock or self.available_stock[product_name] < quantity:
                    return False

                if product_name not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                    self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] = quantity
                else:
                    self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] += quantity

                if product_name not in self.reservations:
                    self.reservations[product_name] = {}
                if user_id not in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    self.reservations[product_name][user_id] = 0
                self.reservations[product_name][user_id] += quantity

                self.available_stock[product_name] -= quantity
                return True

        elif operation == 'remove':
            if quantity <= 0:
                return False

            with self.lock:
                if product_name not in self.users[user_id].cart:
                    return False

                if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] < quantity:
                    return False

                if product_name in self.products:
                    if product_name not in self.available_stock:
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = 0
                    self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                else:
                    if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                        del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                        if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                            del self.reservations[product_name]

                self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] -= quantity
                if self.users[user_id].cart[product_name] <= 0:
                    del self.users[user_id].cart[product_name]
                return True

        return False

    def perform_checkout(self, user_id):
        # Check if user exists
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False, None

        # Get user's cart
        cart = self.users[user_id].cart
        if not cart:
            return False, None

        # Acquire lock for thread-safe operations
        with self.lock:
            # Verify all products in cart are available with matching quantities
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
                if product_name not in self.reservations or user_id not in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    return False, None
                if self.reservations[product_name][user_id] != quantity:
                    return False, None
                # Verify product still exists in products dictionary
                if product_name not in self.products:
                    return False, None

            # Calculate total price
            total_price = sum(
                self.products[product_name].price * quantity
                for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
            )

            # Generate order ID
            order_id = f"{self.order_id_prefix}{str(self.next_order_id).zfill(self.order_id_digits)}"

            # Create order snapshot
            order_snapshot = {
                product_name: {
                    'quantity': quantity,
                    'price_at_checkout': self.products[product_name].price
                }
                for product_name, quantity in cart.items()
            }

            # Create and store order
            order = Order(
                order_id=order_id,
                user_id=user_id,
                total_price=total_price,
                products=order_snapshot
            )
            self.orders[order_id] = order

            # Clear cart and reservations
            for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
                if product_name in self.reservations and user_id in self.reservations[product_name]:
                    del self.reservations[product_name][user_id]
                    if not self.reservations[product_name]:
                        del self.reservations[product_name]

            # Clear the cart
            self.users[user_id].cart.clear()

            # Increment order ID
            self.next_order_id += 1

        return True, order_id

    def update_order_status(self, order_id: str, new_status: str) -> bool:
        # Check if the order exists
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        order = self.orders[order_id]
        current_status = order.status

        # Check if transition is from 'cancelled' (explicitly ignore)
        if current_status == 'cancelled':
            return False

        # Use Order's is_valid_transition method for validation
        if not order.is_valid_transition(new_status):
            return False

        # Handle cancellation separately
        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            with self.lock:
                for product_name, item_data in order.products.items():
                    quantity = item_data['quantity']
                    price_at_checkout = item_data['price_at_checkout']

                    if product_name in self.products:
                        # Product exists, increase available stock
                        self.available_stock[product_name] += quantity
                        self.products[product_name].stock += quantity
                    else:
                        # Product doesn't exist, recreate it with order snapshot details
                        self.products[product_name] = Product(product_name, price_at_checkout, quantity)
                        self.available_stock[product_name] = quantity

        # Update the order status
        order.status = new_status
        return True

    def get_system_state(self):
        """Provides access to the current system state.

        Returns:
            dict: Dictionary containing the current system state with keys:
                - 'users': Current users with user IDs and their carts
                - 'products': Current products with names, prices, and available stock
                - 'available_stock': Products not reserved in any cart
                - 'reservations': All current reservations in the system
                - 'orders': All current orders and their statuses
                - 'next_order_id': The next available order ID
        """
        system_state = {
            "users": {user_id: user.cart for user_id, user in self.users.items()},
            "products": {
                name: {
                    "price": product.price,
                    "stock": product.stock
                } for name, product in self.products.items()
            },
            "available_stock": self.available_stock.copy(),
            "reservations": self.reservations.copy(),
            "orders": {
                order_id: {
                    'status': order.status,
                    'products': order.products,
                    'total_price': order.total_price
                } for order_id, order in self.orders.items()
            },
            "next_order_id": self.next_order_id
        }
        return system_state

def main():
    # Demonstration of the e-shop system
    shop = EShopBackend()

    # Create users
    shop.create_user("user1")
    shop.create_user("user2")

    # Add products
    shop.add_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    shop.add_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)

    # Modify carts
    shop.modify_cart("user1", "Laptop", 2, "add")
    shop.modify_cart("user1", "Phone", 1, "add")
    shop.modify_cart("user2", "Phone", 3, "add")

    # Perform checkout
    success, order_id = shop.perform_checkout("user1")
    if success:
        print(f"Checkout successful! Order ID: {order_id}")
    else:
        print("Checkout failed.")

    # Update order status
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "completed")

    # Delete user
    shop.delete_user("user2")

    # Delete product
    shop.delete_product("Phone")

    # Get system state
    state = shop.get_system_state()
    print("\nCurrent System State:")
    print(state)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== generate_integration_test OUTPUT =====
def test_main_success_scenario():
    shop = EShopBackend()
    # Create user
    assert shop.create_user("user1") is True
    # Add product
    assert shop.add_product("laptop", 999.99, 10) is True
    # Modify cart
    assert shop.modify_cart("user1", "laptop", 2, "add") is True
    # Perform checkout
    success, order_id = shop.perform_checkout("user1")
    assert success is True
    assert order_id == "SK00001"
    # Update order status
    assert shop.update_order_status(order_id, "processing") is True

def test_user_deletion_with_reservations():
    shop = EShopBackend()
    # Create user and add product
    shop.create_user("user2")
    shop.add_product("phone", 599.99, 5)
    # Modify cart and checkout
    shop.modify_cart("user2", "phone", 3, "add")
    success, order_id = shop.perform_checkout("user2")
    # Delete user
    assert shop.delete_user("user2") is True
    # Verify user is deleted
    state = shop.get_system_state()
    assert "user2" not in state["users"]
    # Verify reservations are cleared
    assert "phone" not in state["reservations"] or "user2" not in state["reservations"]["phone"]

def test_product_deletion_with_existing_reservations():
    shop = EShopBackend()
    # Create user and add product
    shop.create_user("user3")
    shop.add_product("tablet", 399.99, 8)
    # Modify cart
    shop.modify_cart("user3", "tablet", 4, "add")
    # Delete product
    assert shop.delete_product("tablet") is True
    # Verify product is deleted
    state = shop.get_system_state()
    assert "tablet" not in state["products"]
    # Verify reservations still exist but don't affect stock
    assert "tablet" in state["reservations"]["user3"]
    assert state["available_stock"]["tablet"] == 8  # Stock remains unchanged

@startuml
start
:initialize e-shop backend system;
:create user1 and user2;
:add Laptop and Phone products;

if (user1's cart not empty) then (yes)
  :verify all reserved products exist and match quantities;
  if (verification successful) then (yes)
    :calculate total price;
    :generate unique order ID;
    :create order with pending status;
    :clear user1's cart and reservations;
    :confirm checkout success;
  else (no)
    :ignore checkout request;
  endif
else (no)
  :ignore checkout request;
endif

:update order status to processing;
:update order status to completed;
:delete user2 and cancel their reservations;
if (product exists) then (yes)
  :return reserved quantity to available stock;
else (no)
  :remove reservation without stock adjustment;
endif

:delete Phone product;
:remove from available stock;
:preserve existing reservations;

:display current system state;
stop

===== OUTPUT_review_activity_diagram OUTPUT =====
Here are the identified defects in the PlantUML activity diagram:

- Missing decision point for checkout failure case where reserved products no longer exist or quantities don't match (the program explicitly checks this in `perform_checkout` but the diagram only shows "verification successful" branch)
- Incorrect order of operations: "update order status to processing" and "update order status to completed" appear as sequential steps without any decision logic, but the program requires explicit status transitions with validation (the diagram implies automatic progression which contradicts the program's `is_valid_transition` checks)
- Missing cancellation handling during order status updates: the diagram doesn't show the stock adjustment logic that occurs when transitioning to "cancelled" status, which is a key behavior in the program
- The "delete user2 and cancel their reservations" step appears after order status updates, but the program's `main()` shows user deletion occurs before product deletion (execution order mismatch)
- Missing atomicity representation: the diagram shows checkout steps as separate actions without indicating they must succeed or fail together (the program implements this via `with self.lock`)
- The "return reserved quantity to available stock" decision appears after user deletion, but the program's `delete_user` method handles this during user deletion (timing mismatch)
- The diagram shows "remove from available stock" after product deletion, but the program's `delete_product` removes it from `available_stock` immediately (incorrect sequence)
===============================

  else (no)

  :handle status update failure;

else (no)
  :remove reservation without stock adjustment;
endif
